round_bit & (sticky_bit | value_before_round[0]);\n    assign value_after_round = value_before_round + round_value;\n\n    logic [n-1:0] normal_result;\n    assign normal_result = sign_i ? {1'b1,~value_after_round+1} : {1'b0,value_after_round};\n    assign result_o = input_not_zero ? normal_result : '0;\n\nendmodule\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Pipelined top\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\nmodule pdpu_top_pipelined #(\n    parameter int unsigned N = 16,\n    parameter int unsigned n_i = 8,\n    parameter int unsigned es_i = 2,\n    parameter int unsigned n_o = 16,\n    parameter int unsigned es_o = 2,\n    parameter int unsigned ALIGN_WIDTH = 14,\n    parameter int unsigned CARRY_W = 5,\n    parameter int unsigned SUM_W = 19\n)(\n    input logic clk,\n    input logic [N*n_i-1:0] operands_a,\n    input logic [N*n_i-1:0] operands_b,\n    input logic [n_o-1:0] acc,\n    output logic [n_o-1:0] result_o\n);\n    localparam int unsigned EXP_WIDTH_I = pdpu_pkg::clog2(n_i-1) + es_i;   // 5\n    localparam int unsigned MANT_WIDTH_I = n_i-es_i-3;                     // 3\n    localparam int unsigned EXP_WIDTH_O = pdpu_pkg::clog2(n_o-1) + es_o;   // 6\n    localparam int unsigned MANT_WIDTH_O = n_o-es_o-3;                     // 11\n    localparam int unsigned EXP_WIDTH = pdpu_pkg::maximum(EXP_WIDTH_I+1,EXP_WIDTH_O); // 6\n    localparam int unsigned MULW = 2*(MANT_WIDTH_I+1);                     // 8 (true product fits)\n    localparam int unsigned SUM_WIDTH = ALIGN_WIDTH + CARRY_W;             // 19\n\n    // ================= Stage 1: decode + multiply =================\n    logic [N-1:0] signs_a, signs_b;\n    logic signed [N*(EXP_WIDTH_I+1)-1:0] rg_exp_a, rg_exp_b;\n    logic [N*(MANT_WIDTH_I+1)-1:0] mants_a, mants_b;\n\n    generate\n        genvar i;\n        for(i=0;i<N;i++) begin: posit_decoding\n            posit_decoder #(.n(n_i),.es(es_i)) ua_posit_decoder (\n                .operand_i(operands_a[(i)*n_i +: n_i]),\n                .sign_o(signs_a[i]),\n                .rg_exp_o(rg_exp_a[(i)*(EXP_WIDTH_I+1) +: (EXP_WIDTH_I+1)]),\n                .mant_norm_o(mants_a[(i)*(MANT_WIDTH_I+1) +: (MANT_WIDTH_I+1)])\n            );\n            posit_decoder #(.n(n_i),.es(es_i)) ub_posit_decoder (\n                .operand_i(operands_b[(i)*n_i +: n_i]),\n                .sign_o(signs_b[i]),\n                .rg_exp_o(rg_exp_b[(i)*(EXP_WIDTH_I+1) +: (EXP_WIDTH_I+1)]),\n                .mant_norm_o(mants_b[(i)*(MANT_WIDTH_I+1) +: (MANT_WIDTH_I+1)])\n            );\n        end\n    endgenerate\n\n    logic sign_acc;\n    logic signed [EXP_WIDTH_O:0] rg_exp_acc;\n    logic [MANT_WIDTH_O:0] mant_norm_acc;\n\n    posit_decoder #(.n(n_o),.es(es_o)) uc_posit_decoder (\n        .operand_i(acc),\n        .sign_o(sign_acc),\n        .rg_exp_o(rg_exp_acc),\n        .mant_norm_o(mant_norm_acc)\n    );\n\n    logic [N-1:0] signs_ab;\n    logic [N:0] s1_signs;\n    assign signs_ab = signs_a ^ signs_b;\n    assign s1_signs = {sign_acc, signs_ab};\n\n    // exponent sums and mantissa products\n    logic signed [N*(EXP_WIDTH+1)-1:0] s1_rg_exp_c;\n    logic [N*MULW-1:0] s1_prod;\n    generate\n        genvar m;\n        for(m=0;m<N;m++) begin\n            assign s1_rg_exp_c[(m)*(EXP_WIDTH+1) +: (EXP_WIDTH+1)] =\n                $signed(rg_exp_a[(m)*(EXP_WIDTH_I+1) +: (EXP_WIDTH_I+1)]) +\n                $signed(rg_exp_b[(m)*(EXP_WIDTH_I+1) +: (EXP_WIDTH_I+1)]);\n            assign s1_prod[(m)*MULW +: MULW] =\n                mants_a[(m)*(MANT_WIDTH_I+1) +: (MANT_WIDTH_I+1)] *\n                mants_b[(m)*(MANT_WIDTH_I+1) +: (MANT_WIDTH_I+1)];\n        end\n    endgenerate\n\n    // balanced popcount of signs (two's complement correction term)\n    logic [1:0] pc1 [0:7];\n    logic [2:0] pc2 [0:3];\n    logic [3:0] pc3 [0:1];\n    logic [4:0] s1_corr;\n    generate\n        genvar p;\n        for(p=0;p<8;p++) begin\n            assign pc1[p] = {1'b0, s1_signs[2*p]} + {1'b0, s1_signs[2*p+1]};\n        end\n        for(p=0;p<4;p++) begin\n            assign pc2[p] = {1'b0, pc1[2*p]} + {1'b0, pc1[2*p+1]};\n        end\n        for(p=0;p<2;p++) begin\n            assign pc3[p] = {1'b0, pc2[2*p]} + {1'b0, pc2[2*p+1]};\n        end\n    endgenerate\n    assign s1_corr = {1'b0, pc3[0]} + {1'b0, pc3[1]} + {4'b0, s1_signs[N]};\n\n    // ---- pipe1 ----\n    logic [N:0] pipe1_signs;\n    logic signed [N*(EXP_WIDTH+1)-1:0] pipe1_rg_exp_c;\n    logic [N*MULW-1:0] pipe1_prod;\n    logic signed [EXP_WIDTH_O:0] pipe1_rg_exp_acc;\n    logic [MANT_WIDTH_O:0] pipe1_mant_norm_acc;\n    logic [4:0] pipe1_corr;\n\n    always_ff @(posedge clk) begin\n        pipe1_signs         <= s1_signs;\n        pipe1_rg_exp_c      <= s1_rg_exp_c;\n        pipe1_prod          <= s1_prod;\n        pipe1_rg_exp_acc    <= rg_exp_acc;\n        pipe1_mant_norm_acc <= mant_norm_acc;\n        pipe1_corr          <= s1_corr;\n    end\n\n    // ============ Stage 2: max-tree, align, sign-XOR ============\n    logic signed [(N+1)*(EXP_WIDTH+1)-1:0] rg_exp_items;\n    generate\n        genvar u;\n        for(u=0;u<N;u++) begin\n            assign rg_exp_items[(u)*(EXP_WIDTH+1) +: (EXP_WIDTH+1)] =\n                pipe1_rg_exp_c[(u)*(EXP_WIDTH+1) +: (EXP_WIDTH+1)];\n        end\n    endgenerate\n    assign rg_exp_items[(N)*(EXP_WIDTH+1) +: (EXP_WIDTH+1)] = $signed(pipe1_rg_exp_acc);\n\n    logic signed [EXP_WIDTH:0] rg_exp_max;\n    comp_tree #(\n        .N(N+1),\n        .WIDTH(EXP_WIDTH)\n    ) u_comp_tree (\n        .operands_i(rg_exp_items),\n        .result_o(rg_exp_max)\n    );\n\n    localparam int unsigned SHIFT_WIDTH = pdpu_pkg::clog2(ALIGN_WIDTH+1);\n    logic [(N+1)*ALIGN_WIDTH-1:0] s2_shifted_xor;\n    generate\n        genvar s;\n        for(s=0;s<N+1;s++) begin : align\n            logic [ALIGN_WIDTH-1:0] product;\n            logic [EXP_WIDTH:0] rg_exp_diff;\n            logic [SHIFT_WIDTH-1:0] shift_amount;\n            logic [ALIGN_WIDTH-1:0] shifted;\n\n            if (s < N) begin\n                if(ALIGN_WIDTH>MULW) begin\n                    assign product = pipe1_prod[(s)*MULW +: MULW] << (ALIGN_WIDTH-MULW);\n                end\n                else begin\n                    assign product = pipe1_prod[(s)*MULW +: MULW] >> (MULW-ALIGN_WIDTH);\n                end\n            end\n            else begin\n                assign product = pipe1_mant_norm_acc << (ALIGN_WIDTH-MANT_WIDTH_O-2);\n            end\n\n            assign rg_exp_diff = $unsigned(rg_exp_max - rg_exp_items[(s)*(EXP_WIDTH+1) +: (EXP_WIDTH+1)]);\n            if(EXP_WIDTH+1>SHIFT_WIDTH) begin\n                assign shift_amount = (|rg_exp_diff[(EXP_WIDTH+1)-1:SHIFT_WIDTH]) ? ALIGN_WIDTH[SHIFT_WIDTH-1:0]\n                                          : rg_exp_diff[SHIFT_WIDTH-1:0];\n            end\n            else begin\n                assign shift_amount = rg_exp_diff[SHIFT_WIDTH-1:0];\n            end\n            assign shifted = product >> shift_amount;\n            assign s2_shifted_xor[(s)*ALIGN_WIDTH +: ALIGN_WIDTH] = shifted ^ {ALIGN_WIDTH{pipe1_signs[s]}};\n        end\n    endgenerate\n\n    // ---- pipe2 ----\n    logic [N:0] pipe2_signs;\n    logic [(N+1)*ALIGN_WIDTH-1:0] pipe2_shifted_xor;\n    logic signed [EXP_WIDTH:0] pipe2_rg_exp_max;\n    logic [4:0] pipe2_corr;\n\n    always_ff @(posedge clk) begin\n        pipe2_signs         <= pipe1_signs;\n        pipe2_shifted_xor   <= s2_shifted_xor;\n        pipe2_rg_exp_max    <= rg_exp_max;\n        pipe2_corr          <= pipe1_corr;\n    end\n\n    // ============ Stage 3: CSA tree + final add ============\n    // value for lane s: sign ? ~(zero-ext z) : zero-ext z, with the\n    // popcount(signs) correction applied in the final add.\n    logic [(N+1)*(SUM_WIDTH+1)-1:0] mantissa_w;\n    generate\n        genvar y;\n        for(y=0;y<N+1;y++) begin\n            assign mantissa_w[(y)*(SUM_WIDTH+1) +: (SUM_WIDTH+1)] =\n                { {(SUM_WIDTH+1-ALIGN_WIDTH){pipe2_signs[y]}}, pipe2_shifted_xor[(y)*ALIGN_WIDTH +: ALIGN_WIDTH] };\n        end\n    endgenerate\n\n    logic [SUM_WIDTH:0] csa_sum, csa_carry;\n    csa_tree #(\n        .N(N+1),\n        .WIDTH_I(SUM_WIDTH+1),\n        .WIDTH_O(SUM_WIDTH+1)\n    ) u_csa_tree (\n        .operands_i(mantissa_w),\n        .sum_o(csa_sum),\n        .carry_o(csa_carry)\n    );\n\n    logic [SUM_WIDTH:0] sum_result;\n    assign sum_result = csa_sum + csa_carry + {(SUM_WIDTH+1-5){1'b0}}, pipe2_corr;\n\n    // ---- pipe3 ----\n    logic [SUM_WIDTH:0] pipe3_sum_result;\n    logic signed [EXP_WIDTH:0] pipe3_rg_exp_max;\n\n    always_ff @(posedge clk) begin\n        pipe3_sum_result <= sum_result;\n        pipe3_rg_exp_max <= pipe2_rg_exp_max;\n    end\n\n    // ============ Stage 4: normalize ============\n    logic final_sign;\n    logic [SUM_WIDTH-1:0] sum_c;\n\n    assign final_sign = pipe3_sum_result[SUM_WIDTH];\n    assign sum_c = final_sign ? (~pipe3_sum_result[SUM_WIDTH-1:0]+1) : pipe3_sum_result[SUM_WIDTH-1:0];\n\n    logic signed [EXP_WIDTH:0] rg_exp_adjust;\n    logic signed [EXP_WIDTH:0] final_rg_exp;\n    logic [SUM_WIDTH-1:0] sum_norm;\n\n    mantissa_norm #(\n        .WIDTH(SUM_WIDTH),\n        .EXP_WIDTH(EXP_WIDTH),\n        .DECIMAL_POINT(CARRY_W+2)\n    ) u_mantissa_norm (\n        .operand_i(sum_c),\n        .exp_adjust(rg_exp_adjust),\n        .result_o(sum_norm)\n    );\n\n    assign final_rg_exp = pipe3_rg_exp_max + rg_exp_adjust;\n\n    logic [MANT_WIDTH_O+2:0] final_mant;\n    if(SUM_WIDTH>MANT_WIDTH_O+3) begin\n        logic [SUM_WIDTH-MANT_WIDTH_O-3:0] sticky_bits;\n        logic sticky_bit;\n        assign sticky_bits = sum_norm[SUM_WIDTH-MANT_WIDTH_O-3:0];\n        assign sticky_bit = |sticky_bits;\n        assign final_mant = {sum_norm[SUM_WIDTH-1:SUM_WIDTH-MANT_WIDTH_O-2],sticky_bit};\n    end\n    else begin\n        assign final_mant = sum_norm << (MANT_WIDTH_O+3-SUM_WIDTH);\n    end\n\n    // ---- pipe4 ----\n    logic pipe4_final_sign;\n    logic signed [EXP_WIDTH:0] pipe4_final_rg_exp;\n    logic [MANT_WIDTH_O+2:0] pipe4_final_mant;\n\n    always_ff @(posedge clk) begin\n        pipe4_final_sign    <= final_sign;\n        pipe4_final_rg_exp  <= final_rg_exp;\n        pipe4_final_mant    <= final_mant;\n    end\n\n    // ============ Stage 5: encode ============\n    logic [n_o-1:0] result_c;\n\n    posit_encoder #(\n        .n(n_o),\n        .es(es_o),\n        .EXP_WIDTH(EXP_WIDTH),\n        .MANT_WIDTH(MANT_WIDTH_O+2)\n    ) u_posit_encoder (\n        .sign_i(pipe4_final_sign),\n        .rg_exp_i(pipe4_final_rg_exp),\n        .mant_norm_i(pipe4_final_mant),\n        .result_o(result_c)\n    );\n\n    // ---- pipe5 ----\n    always_ff @(posedge clk) begin\n        result_o <= result_c;\n    end\n\nendmodule\n")

  -> create_file(content="// PDPU dot-16, 5-stage pipelined -- restructured pipeline v2.\n// Stage plan (5 flop banks, latency 5):\n//   B1: decode 33 posits, 4x4 mantissa multiplies, exponent adds, popcount,\n//       17x exp differences (full) registered\n//   B2: exponent max-tree, 3-bit diff clip + sign-XOR (no subtractors here)\n//   B3: CSA tree over 20-bit lanes + 15-bit final CPA\n//   B4: abs, LZC, normalize shift, sticky/round extract, exp adjust\n//   B5: posit encode -> registered result_o\n\npackage pdpu_pkg;\n    function automatic int maximum(int a, int b);\n        maximum = (a > b) ? a : b;\n    endfunction\n\n    function automatic integer clog2(input integer n);\n        begin\n            n = n-1;\n            for(clog2=0; n>0; clog2=clog2+1)\n                n = n>>1;\n        end\n    endfunction\nendpackage\n\npackage cf_math_pkg;\n    function automatic integer ceil_div (input longint dividend, input longint divisor);\n        longint remainder;\n        remainder = dividend;\n        for (ceil_div = 0; remainder > 0; ceil_div++) begin\n            remainder = remainder - divisor;\n        end\n    endfunction\n\n    function automatic integer clog(input integer n);\n        begin\n            n = n-1;\n            for(clog=0; n>0; clog=clog+1)\n                n = n>>1;\n        end\n    endfunction\n\n    function automatic integer unsigned idx_width (input integer unsigned num_idx);\n        idx_width = (num_idx > 32'd1) ? $unsigned(clog(num_idx)) : 32'd1;\n    endfunction\nendpackage\n\nmodule fulladder(\n    input logic x,y,z,\n    output logic sum,carry\n);\n    assign sum = (x ^ y ) ^ z;\n    assign carry = ((x ^ y) & z) | (x & y);\nendmodule\n\nmodule counter_5to3(\n    input logic x1,x2,x3,x4,cin,\n    output logic sum,carry,cout\n);\n    assign sum = x1 ^ x2 ^ x3 ^ x4 ^ cin;\n    assign cout = (x1 ^ x2) & x3 | ~(x1 ^ x2) & x1;\n    assign carry = (x1 ^ x2 ^ x3 ^ x4) & cin | ~(x1 ^ x2 ^ x3 ^ x4) & x4;\nendmodule\n\nmodule compressor_3to2 #(\n    parameter int unsigned WIDTH_I = 8,\n    parameter int unsigned WIDTH_O = WIDTH_I + pdpu_pkg::clog2(3)\n)(\n    input logic [3*WIDTH_I-1:0] operands_i,\n    output logic [WIDTH_O-1:0] sum_o,\n    output logic [WIDTH_O-1:0] carry_o\n);\n    logic [WIDTH_I-1:0] sum;\n    logic [WIDTH_I-1:0] carry;\n    generate\n        genvar i;\n        for(i=0;i<WIDTH_I;i++) begin\n            fulladder u_fulladder(\n                .x(operands_i[0*WIDTH_I + i]),\n                .y(operands_i[1*WIDTH_I + i]),\n                .z(operands_i[2*WIDTH_I + i]),\n                .sum(sum[i]),\n                .carry(carry[i])\n            );\n        end\n    endgenerate\n\n    assign sum_o = sum;\n    assign carry_o = {1'b0,carry,1'b0};\nendmodule\n\nmodule compressor_4to2 #(\n    parameter int unsigned WIDTH_I = 8,\n    parameter int unsigned WIDTH_O = WIDTH_I + pdpu_pkg::clog2(4)\n)(\n    input logic [4*WIDTH_I-1:0] operands_i,\n    output logic [WIDTH_O-1:0] sum_o,\n    output logic [WIDTH_O-1:0] carry_o\n);\n    logic [WIDTH_I-1:0] sum;\n    logic [WIDTH_I:0] cin;\n    logic [WIDTH_I-1:0] cout;\n    logic [WIDTH_I-1:0] carry;\n\n    assign cin[0] = 1'b0;\n\n    generate\n        genvar i;\n        for(i=0;i<WIDTH_I;i++) begin\n            counter_5to3 u_counter_5to3(\n                .x1(operands_i[0*WIDTH_I + i]),\n                .x2(operands_i[1*WIDTH_I + i]),\n                .x3(operands_i[2*WIDTH_I + i]),\n                .x4(operands_i[3*WIDTH_I + i]),\n                .cin(cin[i]),\n                .sum(sum[i]),\n                .carry(carry[i]),\n                .cout(cout[i])\n            );\n            assign cin[i+1] = cout[i];\n        end\n    endgenerate\n\n    logic [1:0] carry_temp;\n\n    assign sum_o = sum;\n    assign carry_temp = carry[WIDTH_I-1]+cin[WIDTH_I];\n    assign carry_o = {carry_temp,carry[WIDTH_I-2:0],1'b0};\nendmodule\n\nmodule csa_tree #(\n    parameter int unsigned N = 8,\n    parameter int unsigned WIDTH_I = 8,\n    parameter int unsigned WIDTH_O = WIDTH_I + pdpu_pkg::clog2(N)\n)(\n    input logic [N*WIDTH_I-1:0] operands_i,\n    output logic [WIDTH_O-1:0] sum_o,\n    output logic [WIDTH_O-1:0] carry_o\n);\n    localparam int unsigned N_A = N/2;\n    localparam int unsigned N_B = N - N_A;\n\n    generate\n        if (N==1) begin\n            assign sum_o = operands_i[0 +: WIDTH_I];\n            assign carry_o = '0;\n        end\n        else if(N==2) begin\n            assign sum_o = operands_i[0 +: WIDTH_I];\n            assign carry_o = operands_i[WIDTH_I +: WIDTH_I];\n        end\n        else if(N==3) begin\n            compressor_3to2 #(\n                .WIDTH_I(WIDTH_I),\n                .WIDTH_O(WIDTH_O)\n            ) u_compressor_3to2 (\n                .operands_i(operands_i),\n                .sum_o(sum_o),\n                .carry_o(carry_o)\n            );\n        end\n        else if(N==4) begin\n            compressor_4to2 #(\n                .WIDTH_I(WIDTH_I),\n                .WIDTH_O(WIDTH_O)\n            ) u_compressor_4to2 (\n                .operands_i(operands_i),\n                .sum_o(sum_o),\n                .carry_o(carry_o)\n            );\n        end\n        else begin\n            logic [N_A*WIDTH_I-1:0] operands_i_A;\n            logic [N_B*WIDTH_I-1:0] operands_i_B;\n            logic [WIDTH_O-1:0] sum_o_A;\n            logic [WIDTH_O-1:0] sum_o_B;\n            logic [WIDTH_O-1:0] carry_o_A;\n            logic [WIDTH_O-1:0] carry_o_B;\n\n            assign operands_i_A = operands_i[0           +: N_A*WIDTH_I];\n            assign operands_i_B = operands_i[N_A*WIDTH_I +: N_B*WIDTH_I];\n\n            csa_tree #(\n                .N(N_A),\n                .WIDTH_I(WIDTH_I),\n                .WIDTH_O(WIDTH_O)\n            ) ua_csa_tree (\n                .operands_i(operands_i_A),\n                .sum_o(sum_o_A),\n                .carry_o(carry_o_A)\n            );\n\n            csa_tree #(\n                .N(N_B),\n                .WIDTH_I(WIDTH_I),\n                .WIDTH_O(WIDTH_O)\n            ) ub_csa_tree (\n                .operands_i(operands_i_B),\n                .sum_o(sum_o_B),\n                .carry_o(carry_o_B)\n            );\n\n            logic [4*WIDTH_O-1:0] operands_i_C;\n            assign operands_i_C = {sum_o_A, carry_o_A, sum_o_B, carry_o_B};\n\n            compressor_4to2 #(\n                .WIDTH_I(WIDTH_O),\n                .WIDTH_O(WIDTH_O)\n            ) uc_compressor_4to2 (\n                .operands_i(operands_i_C),\n                .sum_o(sum_o),\n                .carry_o(carry_o)\n            );\n        end\n    endgenerate\nendmodule\n\nmodule comparator #(\n    parameter int unsigned WIDTH = 8\n)(\n    input logic signed [WIDTH:0] operand_a,\n    input logic signed [WIDTH:0] operand_b,\n    output logic signed [WIDTH:0] result_o\n);\n    logic [1:0] sign_ab;\n    logic [WIDTH-1:0] data_a;\n    logic [WIDTH-1:0] data_b;\n\n    assign sign_ab = {operand_a[WIDTH],operand_b[WIDTH]};\n    assign data_a = operand_a[WIDTH-1:0];\n    assign data_b = operand_b[WIDTH-1:0];\n\n    always_comb begin\n        case(sign_ab)\n            2'b10: result_o = operand_b;\n            2'b01: result_o = operand_a;\n            default: result_o = (data_a>data_b) ? operand_a : operand_b;\n        endcase\n    end\nendmodule\n\nmodule comp_tree #(\n    parameter N = 4,\n    parameter WIDTH = 8\n)(\n    input logic signed [N*(WIDTH+1)-1:0] operands_i,\n    output logic signed [WIDTH:0] result_o\n);\n    localparam int unsigned W   = WIDTH + 1;\n    localparam int unsigned N_A = N/2;\n    localparam int unsigned N_B = N - N_A;\n\n    generate\n        if (N==1) begin : g_leaf\n            assign result_o = operands_i[0 +: W];\n        end\n        else if (N==2) begin : g_pair\n            comparator #(\n                .WIDTH(WIDTH)\n            ) u_comparator (\n                .operand_a(operands_i[0 +: W]),\n                .operand_b(operands_i[W +: W]),\n                .result_o(result_o)\n            );\n        end\n        else begin : g_split\n            logic signed [N_A*W-1:0] operands_i_A;\n            logic signed [N_B*W-1:0] operands_i_B;\n            logic signed [WIDTH:0]   result_o_A;\n            logic signed [WIDTH:0]   result_o_B;\n\n            assign operands_i_A = operands_i[0       +: N_A*W];\n            assign operands_i_B = operands_i[N_A*W   +: N_B*W];\n\n            comp_tree #(\n                .N(N_A),\n                .WIDTH(WIDTH)\n            ) ua_comp_tree (\n                .operands_i(operands_i_A),\n                .result_o(result_o_A)\n            );\n\n            comp_tree #(\n                .N(N_B),\n                .WIDTH(WIDTH)\n            ) ub_comp_tree (\n                .operands_i(operands_i_B),\n                .result_o(result_o_B)\n            );\n\n            comparator #(\n                .WIDTH(WIDTH)\n            ) uc_comparator (\n                .operand_a(result_o_A),\n                .operand_b(result_o_B),\n                .result_o(result_o)\n            );\n        end\n    endgenerate\nendmodule\n\nmodule barrel_shifter #(\n    parameter int unsigned WIDTH = 8,\n    parameter int unsigned SHIFT_WIDTH = 3,\n    parameter bit MODE = 1'b0\n)(\n    input logic [WIDTH-1:0] operand_i,\n    input logic [SHIFT_WIDTH-1:0] shift_amount,\n    output logic [WIDTH-1:0] result_o\n);\n    logic [SHIFT_WIDTH-1:0][WIDTH-1:0] temp_results;\n\n    assign temp_results[SHIFT_WIDTH-1] = operand_i;\n\n    generate\n        genvar i;\n        if(MODE==1'b0) begin\n            for(i=SHIFT_WIDTH-1;i>0;i--) begin\n                assign temp_results[i-1] = shift_amount[i] ? temp_results[i]<<(2**i) : temp_results[i];\n            end\n            assign result_o = shift_amount[0] ? temp_results[0]<<1 : temp_results;\n        end\n        else begin\n            for(i=SHIFT_WIDTH-1;i>0;i--) begin\n                assign temp_results[i-1] = shift_amount[i] ? temp_results[i]>>(2**i) : temp_results[i];\n            end\n            assign result_o = shift_amount[0] ? temp_results[0]>>1 : temp_results;\n        end\n    endgenerate\nendmodule\n\nmodule lzc #(\n  parameter int unsigned WIDTH = 2,\n  parameter bit          MODE  = 1'b0,\n  parameter int unsigned CNT_WIDTH = cf_math_pkg::idx_width(WIDTH)\n) (\n  input  logic [WIDTH-1:0]     in_i,\n  output logic [CNT_WIDTH-1:0] cnt_o,\n  output logic                 empty_o\n);\n\n  if (WIDTH == 1) begin : gen_degenerate_lzc\n\n    assign cnt_o[0] = !in_i[0];\n    assign empty_o = !in_i[0];\n\n  end else begin : gen_lzc\n\n    localparam int unsigned NumLevels = cf_math_pkg::clog(WIDTH);\n\n    logic [WIDTH*NumLevels-1:0] index_lut;\n    logic [2**NumLevels-1:0] sel_nodes;\n    logic [(2**NumLevels)*NumLevels-1:0] index_nodes;\n\n    logic [WIDTH-1:0] in_tmp;\n\n    always_comb begin : flip_vector\n      for (int unsigned i = 0; i < WIDTH; i++) begin\n        in_tmp[i] = (MODE) ? in_i[WIDTH-1-i] : in_i[i];\n      end\n    end\n\n    for (genvar j = 0; $unsigned(j) < WIDTH; j++) begin : g_index_lut\n      assign index_lut[(j)*NumLevels +: NumLevels] = ($unsigned(j));\n    end\n\n    for (genvar level = 0; $unsigned(level) < NumLevels; level++) begin : g_levels\n      if ($unsigned(level) == NumLevels - 1) begin : g_last_level\n        for (genvar k = 0; k < 2 ** level; k++) begin : g_level\n          if ($unsigned(k) * 2 < WIDTH - 1) begin : g_reduce\n            assign sel_nodes[2 ** level - 1 + k] = in_tmp[k * 2] | in_tmp[k * 2 + 1];\n            assign index_nodes[(2 ** level - 1 + k)*NumLevels +: NumLevels] = (in_tmp[k * 2] == 1'b1)\n              ? index_lut[(k * 2)*NumLevels +: NumLevels] :\n                index_lut[(k * 2 + 1)*NumLevels +: NumLevels];\n          end\n          if ($unsigned(k) * 2 == WIDTH - 1) begin : g_base\n            assign sel_nodes[2 ** level - 1 + k] = in_tmp[k * 2];\n            assign index_nodes[(2 ** level - 1 + k)*NumLevels +: NumLevels] = index_lut[(k * 2)*NumLevels +: NumLevels];\n          end\n          if ($unsigned(k) * 2 > WIDTH - 1) begin : g_out_of_range\n            assign sel_nodes[2 ** level - 1 + k] = 1'b0;\n            assign index_nodes[(2 ** level - 1 + k)*NumLevels +: NumLevels] = '0;\n          end\n        end\n      end else begin : g_not_last_level\n        for (genvar l = 0; l < 2 ** level; l++) begin : g_level\n          assign sel_nodes[2 ** level - 1 + l] =\n              sel_nodes[2 ** (level + 1) - 1 + l * 2] | sel_nodes[2 ** (level + 1) - 1 + l * 2 + 1];\n          assign index_nodes[(2 ** level - 1 + l)*NumLevels +: NumLevels] = (sel_nodes[2 ** (level + 1) - 1 + l * 2] == 1'b1)\n            ? index_nodes[(2 ** (level + 1) - 1 + l * 2)*NumLevels +: NumLevels] :\n              index_nodes[(2 ** (level + 1) - 1 + l * 2 + 1)*NumLevels +: NumLevels];\n        end\n      end\n    end\n\n    assign cnt_o = NumLevels > $unsigned(0) ? index_nodes[(0)*NumLevels +: NumLevels] : {(cf_math_pkg::clog(WIDTH)) {1'b0}};\n    assign empty_o = NumLevels > $unsigned(0) ? ~sel_nodes[0] : ~(|in_i);\n\n  end : gen_lzc\n\nendmodule : lzc\n\nmodule mantissa_norm #(\n    parameter int unsigned WIDTH = 8,\n    parameter int unsigned EXP_WIDTH = 3,\n    parameter int unsigned DECIMAL_POINT = 3\n)(\n    input logic [WIDTH-1:0] operand_i,\n    output logic signed [EXP_WIDTH:0] exp_adjust,\n    output logic [WIDTH-1:0] result_o\n);\n    localparam int unsigned LZC_WIDTH = pdpu_pkg::clog2(WIDTH);\n    logic [LZC_WIDTH-1:0] leading_zero_count;\n    logic lzc_zeroes;\n\n    lzc #(\n        .WIDTH(WIDTH),\n        .MODE(1'b1)\n    ) u_lzc (\n        .in_i(operand_i),\n        .cnt_o(leading_zero_count),\n        .empty_o(lzc_zeroes)\n    );\n\n    always_comb begin\n        if(lzc_zeroes) begin\n            exp_adjust = '0;\n        end\n        else if(leading_zero_count <= DECIMAL_POINT - 1) begin\n            exp_adjust = DECIMAL_POINT - leading_zero_count - 1;\n        end\n        else begin\n            exp_adjust = -$signed(leading_zero_count - DECIMAL_POINT + 1);\n        end\n    end\n\n    barrel_shifter #(\n        .WIDTH(WIDTH),\n        .SHIFT_WIDTH(LZC_WIDTH),\n        .MODE(1'b0)\n    ) u_barrel_shifter (\n        .operand_i(operand_i),\n        .shift_amount(leading_zero_count),\n        .result_o(result_o)\n    );\nendmodule\n\nmodule posit_decoder #(\n    parameter int unsigned n = 16,\n    parameter int unsigned es = 1,\n    parameter int unsigned nd = pdpu_pkg::clog2(n-1),\n    parameter int unsigned EXP_WIDTH = nd+es,\n    parameter int unsigned MANT_WIDTH = n-es-3\n)(\n    input logic [n-1:0] operand_i,\n    output logic sign_o,\n    output logic signed [EXP_WIDTH:0] rg_exp_o,\n    output logic [MANT_WIDTH:0] mant_norm_o\n);\n\n    logic sign;\n    logic [n-2:0] operand_value;\n\n    assign sign = operand_i[n-1];\n    assign operand_value = sign ? (~operand_i[n-2:0]+1) : operand_i[n-2:0];\n\n    logic regS;\n    logic [n-2:0] lzc_operand;\n    logic [nd-1:0] leading_zero_count;\n    logic lzc_zeroes;\n\n    assign regS = operand_value[n-2];\n    assign lzc_operand = regS ? (~operand_value) : operand_value;\n\n    lzc #(\n        .WIDTH(n-1),\n        .MODE(1'b1)\n    ) u_lzc (\n        .in_i(lzc_operand),\n        .cnt_o(leading_zero_count),\n        .empty_o(lzc_zeroes)\n    );\n\n    logic [nd-1:0] runlength;\n    logic [nd-1:0] regime_bits;\n    logic signed [nd:0] regime_k;\n\n    assign runlength = lzc_zeroes ? (n-1) : leading_zero_count;\n    assign regime_bits = lzc_zeroes ? (n-1) : (leading_zero_count+1);\n    assign regime_k = regS ? ({1'b0,runlength-1}) : ({1'b1,~runlength+1});\n\n    logic [n-2:0] op_no_rg;\n\n    barrel_shifter #(\n        .WIDTH(n-1),\n        .SHIFT_WIDTH(nd),\n        .MODE(1'b0)\n    ) u_barrel_shifter (\n        .operand_i(operand_value),\n        .shift_amount(regime_bits),\n        .result_o(op_no_rg)\n    );\n\n    assign sign_o = sign;\n\n    logic [es:0] exp;\n    if(es==0) begin\n        assign exp = 0;\n    end\n    else begin\n        assign exp = op_no_rg[n-2:n-2-es+1];\n    end\n\n    assign rg_exp_o = regime_k << es | exp;\n\n    logic implicit_bit;\n\n    assign implicit_bit = |operand_i[n-2:0];\n    assign mant_norm_o = {implicit_bit, op_no_rg[n-2-es:2]};\nendmodule\n\nmodule posit_encoder #(\n    parameter int unsigned n = 16,\n    parameter int unsigned es = 1,\n    parameter int unsigned nd = pdpu_pkg::clog2(n-1),\n    parameter int unsigned EXP_WIDTH = nd+es,\n    parameter int unsigned MANT_WIDTH = n-es-3\n) (\n    input logic sign_i,\n    input logic signed [EXP_WIDTH:0] rg_exp_i,\n    input logic [MANT_WIDTH:0] mant_norm_i,\n\n    output logic [n-1:0] result_o\n);\n    logic input_not_zero;\n    assign input_not_zero = mant_norm_i[MANT_WIDTH];\n\n    logic signed [EXP_WIDTH-es:0] regime_k;\n    logic signed [es:0] exp;\n\n    assign regime_k = rg_exp_i[EXP_WIDTH:es];\n    if(es==0) begin\n        assign exp = 0;\n    end\n    else begin\n        assign exp = rg_exp_i[es-1:0];\n    end\n\n    logic sign_k;\n    logic [n-2:0] rg_const;\n    logic [n-2:0] regime;\n\n    assign sign_k = rg_exp_i[EXP_WIDTH];\n    assign rg_const = 1;\n    assign regime = sign_k ? rg_const : ~rg_const;\n\n    logic [EXP_WIDTH-es:0] regime_bits;\n\n    assign regime_bits = sign_k ? (~regime_k+2) : (regime_k+2);\n\n    localparam int unsigned TEMP_WIDTH = (n-1)+es+MANT_WIDTH;\n    logic [TEMP_WIDTH-1:0] rg_exp_mant;\n\n    if(es==0) begin\n        assign rg_exp_mant = {regime, mant_norm_i[MANT_WIDTH-1:0]};\n    end\n    else begin\n        assign rg_exp_mant = {regime, exp[es-1:0], mant_norm_i[MANT_WIDTH-1:0]};\n    end\n\n    localparam int unsigned MAX_SHIFT_AMOUNT = MANT_WIDTH + es + 1;\n    localparam int unsigned SHIFT_WIDTH = pdpu_pkg::clog2(MAX_SHIFT_AMOUNT+1);\n    logic [SHIFT_WIDTH-1:0] shift_amount;\n\n    assign shift_amount = (regime_bits>=n) ? MAX_SHIFT_AMOUNT : (regime_bits+(MANT_WIDTH+es-n+1));\n\n    logic [TEMP_WIDTH+MAX_SHIFT_AMOUNT-1:0] value_before_shift, value_after_shift;\n\n    assign value_before_shift = rg_exp_mant << MAX_SHIFT_AMOUNT;\n    barrel_shifter #(\n        .WIDTH(TEMP_WIDTH+MAX_SHIFT_AMOUNT),\n        .SHIFT_WIDTH(SHIFT_WIDTH),\n        .MODE(1'b1)\n    ) u_barrel_shifter (\n        .operand_i(value_before_shift),\n        .shift_amount(shift_amount),\n        .result_o(value_after_shift)\n    );\n\n    logic [n-2:0] value_before_round;\n    logic [MAX_SHIFT_AMOUNT-1:0] rounding_bits;\n\n    assign {value_before_round,rounding_bits} = value_after_shift[MAX_SHIFT_AMOUNT+n-2:0];\n\n    logic round_bit;\n    logic sticky_bit;\n    logic round_value;\n    logic [n-2:0] value_after_round;\n\n    assign round_bit = rounding_bits[MAX_SHIFT_AMOUNT-1];\n    assign sticky_bit = |rounding_bits[MAX_SHIFT_AMOUNT-2:0];\n    assign round_value = round_bit & (sticky_bit | value_before_round[0]);\n    assign value_after_round = value_before_round + round_value;\n\n    logic [n-1:0] normal_result;\n    assign normal_result = sign_i ? {1'b1,~value_after_round+1} : {1'b0,value_after_round};\n    assign result_o = input_not_zero ? normal_result : '0;\n\nendmodule\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Pipelined top\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\nmodule pdpu_top_pipelined #(\n    parameter int unsigned N = 16,\n    parameter int unsigned n_i = 8,\n    parameter int unsigned es_i = 2,\n    parameter int unsigned n_o = 16,\n    parameter int unsigned es_o = 2,\n    parameter int unsigned ALIGN_WIDTH = 14,\n    parameter int unsigned CARRY_W = 5,\n    parameter int unsigned SUM_W = 19\n)(\n    input logic clk,\n    input logic [N*n_i-1:0] operands_a,\n    input logic [N*n_i-1:0] operands_b,\n    input logic [n_o-1:0] acc,\n    output logic [n_o-1:0] result_o\n);\n    localparam int unsigned EXP_WIDTH_I = pdpu_pkg::clog2(n_i-1) + es_i;   // 5\n    localparam int unsigned MANT_WIDTH_I = n_i-es_i-3;                     // 3\n    localparam int unsigned EXP_WIDTH_O = pdpu_pkg::clog2(n_o-1) + es_o;   // 6\n    localparam int unsigned MANT_WIDTH_O = n_o-es_o-3;                     // 11\n    localparam int unsigned EXP_WIDTH = pdpu_pkg::maximum(EXP_WIDTH_I+1,EXP_WIDTH_O); // 6\n    localparam int unsigned MULW = 2*(MANT_WIDTH_I+1);                     // 8\n    localparam int unsigned SUM_WIDTH = ALIGN_WIDTH + CARRY_W;             // 19\n    localparam int unsigned SHIFT_WIDTH = pdpu_pkg::clog2(ALIGN_WIDTH+1);  // 4\n    localparam int unsigned DIFF_W = pdpu_pkg::maximum(EXP_WIDTH+2, SHIFT_WIDTH+1); // 7\n    localparam int unsigned CSA_W = ALIGN_WIDTH + 1;                       // 15 (upper sum bits unused)\n\n    // ================= Stage 1: decode + multiply + exp diffs =================\n    logic [N-1:0] signs_a, signs_b;\n    logic signed [N*(EXP_WIDTH_I+1)-1:0] rg_exp_a, rg_exp_b;\n    logic [N*(MANT_WIDTH_I+1)-1:0] mants_a, mants_b;\n\n    generate\n        genvar i;\n        for(i=0;i<N;i++) begin: posit_decoding\n            posit_decoder #(.n(n_i),.es(es_i)) ua_posit_decoder (\n                .operand_i(operands_a[(i)*n_i +: n_i]),\n                .sign_o(signs_a[i]),\n                .rg_exp_o(rg_exp_a[(i)*(EXP_WIDTH_I+1) +: (EXP_WIDTH_I+1)]),\n                .mant_norm_o(mants_a[(i)*(MANT_WIDTH_I+1) +: (MANT_WIDTH_I+1)])\n            );\n            posit_decoder #(.n(n_i),.es(es_i)) ub_posit_decoder (\n                .operand_i(operands_b[(i)*n_i +: n_i]),\n                .sign_o(signs_b[i]),\n                .rg_exp_o(rg_exp_b[(i)*(EXP_WIDTH_I+1) +: (EXP_WIDTH_I+1)]),\n                .mant_norm_o(mants_b[(i)*(MANT_WIDTH_I+1) +: (MANT_WIDTH_I+1)])\n            );\n        end\n    endgenerate\n\n    logic sign_acc;\n    logic signed [EXP_WIDTH_O:0] rg_exp_acc;\n    logic [MANT_WIDTH_O:0] mant_norm_acc;\n\n    posit_decoder #(.n(n_o),.es(es_o)) uc_posit_decoder (\n        .operand_i(acc),\n        .sign_o(sign_acc),\n        .rg_exp_o(rg_exp_acc),\n        .mant_norm_o(mant_norm_acc)\n    );\n\n    logic [N-1:0] signs_ab;\n    logic [N:0] s1_signs;\n    assign signs_ab = signs_a ^ signs_b;\n    assign s1_signs = {sign_acc, signs_ab};\n\n    // exponent sums\n    logic signed [N*(EXP_WIDTH+1)-1:0] s1_rg_exp_c;\n    generate\n        genvar m;\n        for(m=0;m<N;m++) begin\n            assign s1_rg_exp_c[(m)*(EXP_WIDTH+1) +: (EXP_WIDTH+1)] =\n                $signed(rg_exp_a[(m)*(EXP_WIDTH_I+1) +: (EXP_WIDTH_I+1)]) +\n                $signed(rg_exp_b[(m)*(EXP_WIDTH_I+1) +: (EXP_WIDTH_I+1)]);\n        end\n    endgenerate\n\n    // mantissa products (true product, no carries)\n    logic [N*MULW-1:0] s1_prod;\n    generate\n        genvar q;\n        for(q=0;q<N;q++)      assign pc3[4*p +: 4] = {1'b0, pc2[6*p +: 3]} + {1'b0, pc2[6*p+3 +: 3]};\n        end\n    endgenerate\n    assign s1_corr = {1'b0, pc3[3:0]} + {1'b0, pc3[7:4]} + {4'b0, s1_signs[N]};\n\n    // operand_i,\n    output logic signed [EXP_WIDTH:0] exp_adjust,\n    output logic [WIDTH-1:0] result_o\n);\n    localparam int unsigned LZC_WIDTH = pdpu_pkg::clog2(WIDTH);\n    logic [LZC_WIDTH-1:0] .empty_o(lzc_zeroes)\n    );\n\n    always_comb begin\n        if(lzc_zeroes) begin\n            exp_adjust = '0;\n        end\n        else if(leading_zero_count <= DECIMAL_POINT - 1) begin\n            exp_adjust = DECIMAL_POINT - leading_zero_count - 1;\n        end\n        else begin\n            exp_adjust = -$signed(leading_zero_count - DECIMAL_POINT + 1);\n        end\n    end\n\n    barrel_shifter #(\n        .WIDTH(WIDTH),\n        .SHIFT_WIDTH(LZC_WIDTH),\n        .MODE(1'b0)\n    ) u_barrel_shifter (\n        .operand_i(operand_i),\n        .shift_amount(leading_zero_count),\n        .result_o(result_o)\n    );\nendmodule\n\nmodule posit_decoder #(\n    parameter int unsigned n = 16,\n    parameter int unsigned es = 1,\n    parameter int unsigned nd = (~operand_value) : operand_value;\n\n    lzc #(\n        .WIDTH(n-1),\n        .MODE(1'b1)\n    ) u_lzc (\n        .in_i(lzc_operand),\n        .cnt_o(leading_zero_count),\n        .empty_o(lzc_zeroes)\n    );\n\n    logic [nd-1:0] runlength;\n    logic [nd-1:0] regime_bits;\n    logic signed [nd:0] regime_k;\n\n    assign runlength = lzc_zeroes ? (n-1) : leading_zero_count;\n    assign regime_bits = lzc_zeroes ? (n-1) : (leading_zero_count+1);\n    assign regime_k = regS ? ({1'b0,runlength-1}) : ({1'b1,~runlength+1});\n\n    logic [n-2:0] op_no_rg;\n\n    barrel_shifter #(\n        .WIDTH(n-1),\n        .SHIFT_WIDTH(nd),\n        .MODE(1'b0)\n    ) u_barrel_shifter (\n        .operand_i(operand_value),\n        .shift_amount(regime_bits),\n        .result_o(op_no_rg)\n    );\n\n    assign sign_o = sign;\n\n    logic [es:0] exp;\n    if(es==0) begin\n        assign exp = 0;\n    end\n    else begin\n        assign exp = op_no_rg[n-2:n-2-es+1];\n    end\n\n    assign rg_exp_o = regime_k << es | exp;\n\n    logic implicit_bit;\n\n    assign implicit_bit = |operand_i[n-2:0];\n    assign mant_norm_o = {implicit_bit, op_no_rg[n-2-es:2]};\nendmodule\n\n// posit(16,2) encoder, window-shift formulation (bit-identical to upstream):\n//   vbr  = rg_exp_mant >> d      (15 LSBs of shifted 46-bit window)\n//   rbit = d>0  ? rg_exp_mant[d-1]   : 0\n//   sticky = d>=2 ? |rg_exp_mant[d-2:0] : 0\n// with d = min(regime_bits, 16).\nmodule posit_encoder #(\n    parameter int unsigned n = 16,\n    parameter int unsigned es = 1,\n    parameter int unsigned nd = pdpu_pkg::clog2(n-1),\n    parameter int unsigned EXP_WIDTH = nd+es,\n    parameter int unsigned MANT_WIDTH = n-es-3\n) (\n    input logic sign_i,\n    input logic signed [EXP_WIDTH:0] rg_exp_i,\n    input logic [MANT_WIDTH:0] mant_norm_i,\n\n    output logic [n-1:0] result_o\n);\n    logic input_not_zero;\n    assign input_not_zero = mant_norm_i[MANT_WIDTH];\n\n    logic signed [EXP_WIDTH-es:0] regime_k;\n    logic signed [es:0] exp;\n\n    assign regime_k = rg_exp_i[EXP_WIDTH:es];\n    if(es==0) begin\n        assign exp = 0;\n    end\n    else begin\n        assign exp = rg_exp_i[es-1:0];\n    end\n\n    logic sign_k;\n    logic [n-2:0] regime;\n\n    assign sign_k = rg_exp_i[EXP_WIDTH];\n    assign regime = sign_k ? {{(n-2){1'b0}},1'b1} : {{(n-2){1'b1}},1'b0};\n\n    logic [EXP_WIDTH-es+1:0] regime_bits; // one extra bit for overflow detect\n\n    assign regime_bits = sign_k ? (~regime_k+2) : (regime_k+2);\n\n    localparam int unsigned TEMP_WIDTH = (n-1)+es+MANT_WIDTH;\n    logic [TEMP_WIDTH-1:0] rg_exp_mant;\n\n    if(es==0) begin\n        assign rg_exp_mant = {regime, mant_norm_i[MANT_WIDTH-1:0]};\n    end\n    else begin\n        assign rg_exp_mant = {regime, exp[es-1:0], mant_norm_i[MANT_WIDTH-1:0]};\n    end\n\n    localparam int unsigned MAX_SHIFT_AMOUNT = MANT_WIDTH + es + 1;\n    localparam int unsigned SHIFT_WIDTH = pdpu_pkg::clog2(MAX_SHIFT_AMOUNT+1);\n    logic [SHIFT_WIDTH-1:0] shift_amount;\n\n    assign shift_amount = (regime_bits>=n) ? MAX_SHIFT_AMOUNT[SHIFT_WIDTH-1:0]\n                                           : (regime_bits[SHIFT_WIDTH-1:0]+(MANT_WIDTH+es-n+1));\n\n    // Window extraction: value_after_shift = (rg_exp_mant << MAX_SHIFT_AMOUNT) >> shift_amount\n    // window bits [MAX+n-2:0]; only those matter. Equivalent to shifting\n    // rg_exp_mant right by d = MAX_SHIFT_AMOUNT - shift_amount (>=0), then\n    // taking bits [n-2:0] of the TEMP_WIDTH-wide result... we implement\n    // directly on the MAX_SHIFT_AMOUNT;\n    barrel_shifter #(\n        .WIDTH(TEMP_WIDTH+MAX_SHIFT_AMOUNT),\n        .SHIFT_WIDTH(SHIFT_WIDTH),\n        .MODE(1'b1)\n    ) u_barrel_shifter (\n        .operand_i(value_before_shift),\n        .shift_amount(shift_amount),\n        .result_o(value_after_shift)\n    );\n\n    logic [n-2:0] value_before_round;\n    logic [MAX_SHIFT_AMOUNT-1:0] rounding_bits;\n\n    assign {value_before_round,rounding_bits} = value_after_shift[MAX_SHIFT_AMOUNT+n-2:0];\n\n    logic round_bit;\n    logic sticky_bit;\n    logic round_value;\n    logic [n-2:0] value_after_round;\n\n    assign round_bit = rounding_bits[MAX_SHIFT_AMOUNT-1];\n    assign sticky_bit = |rounding_bits[MAX_SHIFT_AMOUNT-2:0];\n    assign round_value = round_bit & (sticky_bit | value_before_round[0]);\n    assign value_after_round = value_before_round + round_value;\n\n    logic [n-1:0] normal_result;\n    assign normal_result = sign_i ? {1'b1,~value_after_round+1} : {1'b0,value_after_round};\n    assign result_o = input_not_zero ? normal_result : '0;\n\nendmodule\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Pipelined top\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\nmodule pdpu_top_pipelined #(\n    parameter int unsigned N = 16,\n    parameter int unsigned n_i = 8,\n    parameter int unsigned es_i = 2,\n    parameter int unsigned n_o = 16,\n    parameter int unsigned es_o = 2,\n    parameter int unsigned ALIGN_WIDTH = 14,\n    parameter int unsigned CARRY_W = 5,\n   -> create_file(content="// PDPU dot-16, 5-stage pipelined -- restructured pipeline v4.\n// Baseline partition (decode+adds+max+mult | sub+align | CSA+CPA | norm | enc)\n// plus: true 8-bit products .operand_i(value_before_shift),\n        .shift_amount(shift_amount),\n        .result_o(value_after_shift)\n    );\n\n    logic [n-2:0] value_before_round;\n    logic [MAX_SHIFT_AMOUNT-1:0] rounding_bits;\n\n    assign {value_before_round,rounding_bits} = value_after_shift[MAX_SHIFT_AMOUNT+n-2:0];\n\n    logic round_bit;\n    logic sticky_bit;\n    logic round_value;\n    logic [n-2:0] value_after_round;\n\n    assign round_bit = rounding_bits[MAX_SHIFT_AMOUNT-1];\n    assign sticky_bit = |rounding_bits[MAX_SHIFT_AMOUNT-2:0];\n    assign round_value = round_bit & (sticky_bit | value_before_round[0]);\n    assign value_after_round = value_before_round + round_value;\n\n    logic [n-1:0] normal_result;\n    assign normal_result = sign_i ? {1'b1,~value_after_round+1} : {1'b0,value_after_round};\n    assign result_o = input_not_zero ? normal_result : '0;\n\nendmodule\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Pipelined top\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\nmodule pdpu_top_pipelined #(\n    parameter int unsigned N = 16,\n    parameter int unsigned n_i = 8,\n    parameter int unsigned es_i = 2,\n    parameter int unsigned n_o = 16,\n    parameter int unsigned es_o = 2,\n    parameter int unsigned ALIGN_WIDTH = 14,\n    parameter int unsigned CARRY_W = 5,\n    parameter int unsigned SUM_W = 19\n)(\n    input logic clk,\n    input logic [N*n_i-1:0] operands_a,\n    input logic [N*n_i-1:0] operands_b,\n    input logic [n_o-1:0] acc,\n    output logic [n_o-1:0] result_o\n);\n    localparam int unsigned EXP_WIDTH_I = pdpu_pkg::clog2(n_i-1) + es_i;   // 5\n    localparam int unsigned MANT_WIDTH_I = n_i-es_i-3;                     // 3\n    localparam int unsigned EXP_WIDTH_O = pdpu_pkg::clog2(n_o-1) + es_o;   // 6\n    localparam int unsigned MANT_WIDTH_O = n_o-es_o-3;                     // 11\n    localparam int unsigned EXP_WIDTH = pdpu_pkg::maximum(EXP_WIDTH_I+1,EXP_WIDTH_O); // 6\n    localparam int unsigned MULW = 2*(MANT_WIDTH_I+1);                     // 8\n    localparam int unsigned SUM_WIDTH = ALIGN_WIDTH + CARRY_W;             // 19\n    localparam int unsigned SHIFT_WIDTH = pdpu_pkg::clog2(ALIGN_WIDTH+1);  // 4\n    localparam int unsigned LANE_W = SUM_WIDTH;                            // 19\n\n    // ================= Stage 1: decode, multiply, max tree =================\n    logic [N-1:0] signs_a, signs_b;\n    logic signed [N*(EXP_WIDTH_I+1)-1:0] rg_exp_a, rg_exp_b;\n    logic [N*(MANT_WIDTH_I+1)-1:0] mants_a, mants_b;\n\n    generate\n        genvar i;\n        for(i=0;i<N;i++) begin: posit_decoding\n            posit_decoder #(.n(n_i),.es(es_i)) ua_posit_decoder (\n                .operand_i(operands_a[(i)*n_i +: n_i]),\n                .sign_o(signs_a[i]),\n                .rg_exp_o(rg_exp_a[(i)*(EXP_WIDTH_I+1) +: (EXP_WIDTH_I+1)]),\n                .mant_norm_o(mants_a[(i)*(MANT_WIDTH_I+1) +: (MANT_WIDTH_I+1)])\n            );\n            posit_decoder #(.n(n_i),.es(es_i)) ub_posit_decoder (\n                .operand_i(operands_b[(i)*n_i +: n_i]),\n                .sign_o(signs_b[i]),\n                .rg_exp_o(rg_exp_b[(i)*(EXP_WIDTH_I+1) +: (EXP_WIDTH_I+1)]),\n                .mant_norm_o(mants_b[(i)*(MANT_WIDTH_I+1) +: (MANT_WIDTH_I+1)])\n            );\n        end\n    endgenerate\n\n    logic sign_acc;\n    logic signed [EXP_WIDTH_O:0] rg_exp_acc;\n    logic [MANT_WIDTH_O:0] mant_norm_acc;\n\n    posit_decoder #(.n(n_o),.es(es_o)) uc_posit_decoder (\n        .operand_i(acc),\n        .sign_o(sign_acc),\n        .rg_exp_o(rg_exp_acc),\n        .mant_norm_o(mant_norm_acc)\n    );\n\n    logic [N-1:0] signs_ab;\n    logic [N:0] s1_signs;\n    assign signs_ab = signs_a ^ signs_b;\n    assign s1_signs = {sign_acc, signs_ab};\n\n    // exponent sums and mantissa products\n    logic signed [N*(EXP_WIDTH+1)-1:0] s1_rg_exp_c;\n    logic [N*MULW-1:0] s1_prod;\n    generate\n        genvar m;\n        for(m=0;m<N;m++) begin\n            assign s1_rg_exp_c[(m)*(EXP_WIDTH+1) +: (EXP_WIDTH+1)] =\n                $signed(rg_exp_a[(m)*(EXP_WIDTH_I+1) +: (EXP_WIDTH_I+1)]) +\n                $signed(rg_exp_b[(m)*(EXP_WIDTH_I+1) +: (EXP_WIDTH_I+1)]);\n            assign s1_prod[(m)*MULW +: MULW] =\n                mants_a[(m)*(MANT_WIDTH_I+1) +: (MANT_WIDTH_I+1)] *\n                mants_b[(m)*(MANT_WIDTH_I+1) +: (MANT_WIDTH_I+1)];\n        end\n    endgenerate\n\n    // exponent items (17 x 7 bits) and max tree\n    logic signed [(N+1)*(EXP_WIDTH+1)-1:0] rg_exp_items;\n    generate\n        genvar t;\n        for(t=0;t<N;t++) begin\n            assign rg_exp_items[(t)*(EXP_WIDTH+1) +: (EXP_WIDTH+1)] =\n                s1_rg_exp_c[(t)*(EXP_WIDTH+1) +: (EXP_WIDTH+1)];\n        end\n    endgenerate\n    assign rg_exp_items[(N)*(EXP_WIDTH+1) +: (EXP_WIDTH+1)] = $signed(rg_exp_acc);\n\n    logic signed [EXP_WIDTH:0] rg_exp_max;\n    comp_tree #(\n        .N(N+1),\n        .WIDTH(EXP_WIDTH)\n    ) u_comp_tree (\n        .operands_i(rg_exp_items),\n        .result_o(rg_exp_max)\n    );\n\n    // balanced popcount of signs (two's complement correction term)\n    logic [15:0] pc1;\n    logic [11:0] pc2;\n    logic [7:0]  pc3;\n    logic [4:0] s1_corr;\n    generate\n        genvar p;\n        for(p=0;p<8;p++) begin\n            assign pc1[2*p +: 2] = {1'b0, s1_signs[2*p]} + {1'b0, s1_signs[2*p+1]};\n        end\n        for(p=0;p<4;p++) begin\n            assign pc2[3*p +: 3] = {1'b0, pc1[4*p +: 2]} + {1'b0, pc1[4*p+2 +: 2]};\n        end\n        for(p=0;p<2;p++) begin\n            assign pc3[4*p +: 4] = {1'b0, pc2[6*p +: 3]} + {1'b0, pc2[6*p+3 +: 3]};\n        end\n    endgenerate\n    assign s1_corr = {1'b0, pc3[3:0]} + {1'b0, pc3[7:4]} + {4'b0, s1_signs[N]};\n\n    // ---- pipe1 ----\n    logic [N:0] pipe1_signs;\n    logic signed [(N+1)*(EXP_WIDTH+1)-1:0] pipe1_rg_exp_items;\n    logic signed [EXP_WIDTH:0] pipe1_rg_exp_max;\n    logic [N*MULW-1:0] pipe1_prod;\n    logic [MANT_WIDTH_O:0] pipe1_mant_norm_acc;\n    logic [4:0] pipe1_corr;\n\n    always_ff @(posedge clk) begin\n        pipe1_signs         <= s1_signs;\n        pipe1_rg_exp_items  <= rg_exp_items;\n        pipe1_rg_exp_max    <= rg_exp_max;\n        pipe1_prod          <= s1_prod;\n        pipe1_mant_norm_acc <= mant_norm_acc;\n        pipe1_corr          <= s1_corr;\n    end\n\n    // ============ Stage 2: diff + align + sign-XOR ============\n    logic [(N+1)*ALIGN_WIDTH-1:0] s2_shifted_xor;\n    generate\n        genvar s;\n        for(s=0;s<N+1;s++) begin : align\n            logic [ALIGN_WIDTH-1:0] product;\n            logic [EXP_WIDTH:0] rg_exp_diff;\n            logic [SHIFT_WIDTH-1:0] shift_amount;\n            logic [ALIGN_WIDTH-1:0] shifted;\n\n            if (s < N) begin\n                assign product = pipe1_prod[(s)*MULW +: MULW] << (ALIGN_WIDTH-MULW);\n            end\n            else begin\n                assign product = pipe1_mant_norm_acc << (ALIGN_WIDTH-MANT_WIDTH_O-2);\n            end\n\n            assign rg_exp_diff = $unsigned(pipe1_rg_exp_max - pipe1_rg_exp_items[(s)*(EXP_WIDTH+1) +: (EXP_WIDTH+1)]);\n            if(EXP_WIDTH+1>SHIFT_WIDTH) begin\n                assign shift_amount = (|rg_exp_diff[(EXP_WIDTH+1)-1:SHIFT_WIDTH]) ? ALIGN_WIDTH[SHIFT_WIDTH-1:0]\n                                                                                    : rg_exp_diff[SHIFT_WIDTH-1:0];\n            end\n            else begin\n                assign shift_amount = rg_exp_diff[SHIFT_WIDTH-1:0];\n            end\n            assign shifted = product >> shift_amount;\n            assign s2_shifted_xor[(s)*ALIGN_WIDTH +: ALIGN_WIDTH] = shifted ^ {ALIGN_WIDTH{pipe1_signs[s]}};\n        end\n    endgenerate\n\n    // ---- pipe2 ----\n    logic [N:0] pipe2_signs;\n    logic [(N+1)*ALIGN_WIDTH-1:0] pipe2_shifted_xor;\n    logic signed [EXP_WIDTH:0] pipe2_rg_exp_max;\n    logic [4:0] pipe2_corr;\n\n    always_ff @(posedge clk) begin\n        pipe2_signs         <= pipe1_signs;\n        pipe2_shifted_xor   <= s2_shifted_xor;\n        pipe2_rg_exp_max    <= pipe1_rg_exp_max;\n        pipe2_corr          <= pipe1_corr;\n    end\n\n    // ============ Stage 3: CSA tree + 19-bit final add ============\n    logic [(N+1)*LANE_W-1:0] mantissa_w;\n    generate\n        genvar y;\n        for(y=0;y<N+1;y++) begin\n            assign mantissa_w[(y)*LANE_W +: LANE_W] =\n                { {(LANE_W-ALIGN_WIDTH){pipe2_signs[y]}}, pipe2_shifted_xor[(y)*ALIGN_WIDTH +: ALIGN_WIDTH] };\n        end\n    endgenerate\n\n    logic [LANE_W-1:0] csa_sum, csa_carry;\n    csa_tree #(\n        .N(N+1),\n        .WIDTH_I(LANE_W),\n        .WIDTH_O(LANE_W)\n    ) u_csa_tree (\n        .operands_i(mantissa_w),\n        .sum_o(csa_sum),\n        .carry_o(csa_carry)\n    );\n\n    logic [LANE_W-1:0] sum_result;\n    assign sum_result = csa_sum + csa_carry + {{(LANE_W-5){1'b0}}, pipe2_corr};\n\n    // ---- pipe3 ----\n    logic [LANE_W-1:0] pipe3_sum_result;\n    logic signed [EXP_WIDTH:0] pipe3_rg_exp_max;\n\n    always_ff @(posedge clk) begin\n        pipe3_sum_result <= sum_result;\n        pipe3_rg_exp_max <= pipe2_rg_exp_max;\n    end\n\n    // ============ Stage 4: normalize ============\n    logic final_sign;\n    logic [SUM_WIDTH-1:0] sum_c;\n\n    assign final_sign = pipe3_sum_result[LANE_W-1];\n    assign sum_c = final_sign ? (~pipe3_sum_result+1) : pipe3_sum_result;\n\n    logic signed [EXP_WIDTH:0] rg_exp_adjust;\n    logic signed [EXP_WIDTH:0] final_rg_exp;\n    logic [SUM_WIDTH-1:0] sum_norm;\n\n    mantissa_norm #(\n        .WIDTH(SUM_WIDTH),\n        .EXP_WIDTH(EXP_WIDTH),\n        .DECIMAL_POINT(CARRY_W+2)\n    ) u_mantissa_norm (\n        .operand_i(sum_c),\n        .exp_adjust(rg_exp_adjust),\n        .result_o(sum_norm)\n    );\n\n    assign final_rg_exp = pipe3_rg_exp_max + rg_exp_adjust;\n\n    logic [MANT_WIDTH_O+2:0] final_mant;\n    if(SUM_WIDTH>MANT_WIDTH_O+3) begin\n        logic [SUM_WIDTH-MANT_WIDTH_O-3:0] sticky_bits;\n        logic sticky_bit;\n        assign sticky_bits = sum_norm[SUM_WIDTH-MANT_WIDTH_O-3:0];\n        assign sticky_bit = |sticky_bits;\n        assign final_mant = {sum_norm[SUM_WIDTH-1:SUM_WIDTH-MANT_WIDTH_O-2],sticky_bit};\n    end\n    else begin\n        assign final_mant = sum_norm << (MANT_WIDTH_O+3-SUM_WIDTH);\n    end\n\n    // ---- pipe4 ----\n    logic pipe4_final_sign;\n    logic signed [EXP_WIDTH:0] pipe4_final_rg_exp;\n    logic [MANT_WIDTH_O+2:0] pipe4_final_mant;\n\n    always_ff @(posedge clk) begin\n        pipe4_final_sign    <= final_sign;\n        pipe4_final_rg_exp  <= final_rg_exp;\n        pipe4_final_mant    <= final_mant;\n    end\n\n    // ============ Stage 5: encode ============\n    logic [n_o-1:0] result_c;\n\n    posit_encoder #(\n        .n(n_o),\n        .es(es_o),\n        .EXP_WIDTH(EXP_WIDTH),\n        .MANT_WIDTH(MANT_WIDTH_O+2)\n    ) u_posit_encoder (\n        .sign_i(pipe4_final_sign),\n        .rg_exp_i(pipe4_final_rg_exp),\n        .mant_norm_i(pipe4_final_mant),\n        .result_o(result_c)\n    );\n\n    // ---- pipe5 ----\n    always_ff @(posedge clk) begin\n        result_o <= result_c;\n    end\n\nendmodule\n", filename='design_v4.v')

[ASSISTANT]
The critical path moved to stage 1 (decode+max-tree+muls = 1419ps). Key insight: the baseline keeps the **encoder combinational after pipe5** (off the critical path), and splits mults/max-tree into stage 2 while subtract+align is stage 3. Let me adopt the baseline partition but keep my optimizations:


  -> create_file(content="// PDPU dot-16, 5-stage pipelined -- v5.\n// Baseline pipeline partition + optimizations:\n//   * true 8-bit products via `*` (no booth/CSA), 128 instead of 256 reg bits\n//   * sign-XOR trick: register shifted^sign (14 bits + sign) instead of\n//     20-bit negated lanes; popcount(signs) correction added in the final CPA\n//   * 19-bit lanes / CPA instead of 20\n//   * encoder stays combinational after pipe5 (off reg-to-reg timing paths)\n//\n// Stages (latency 5):\n//   S1: decode 33 posits, exponent adds, popcount         -> pipe1\n//   S2: 4x4 multiplies, exponent max-tree                 -> pipe2\n//   S3: diff subtract+clip, align right-shift, sign-XOR   -> pipe3\n//   S4: sign-extend, CSA tree, 19-bit CPA + correction    -> pipe4\n//   S5: abs, LZC, normalize, sticky/round, exp adjust     -> pipe5\n//   encoder: pipe5 -> result_o (combinational)\n\npackage pdpu_pkg;\n    function automatic int maximum(int a, int b);\n        maximum = (a > b) ? a : b;\n    endfunction\n\n    function automatic integer clog2(input integer n);\n        begin\n            n = n-1;\n            for(clog2=0; n>0; clog2=clog2+1)\n                n = n>>1;\n        end\n    endfunction\nendpackage\n\npackage cf_math_pkg;\n    function automatic integer ceil_div (input longint dividend, input longint divisor);\n        longint remainder;\n        remainder = dividend;\n        for (ceil_div = 0; remainder > 0; ceil_div++) begin\n            remainder = remainder - divisor;\n        end\n    endfunction\n\n    function automatic integer clog(input integer n);\n        begin\n            n = n-1;\n            for(clog=0; n>0; clog=clog+1)\n                n = n>>1;\n        end\n    endfunction\n\n    function automatic integer unsigned idx_width (input integer unsigned num_idx);\n        idx_width = (num_idx > 32'd1) ? $unsigned(clog(num_idx)) : 32'd1;\n    endfunction\nendpackage\n\nmodule fulladder(\n    input logic x,y,z,\n    output logic sum,carry\n);\n    assign sum = (x ^ y ) ^ z;\n    assign carry = ((x ^ y) & z) | (x & y);\nendmodule\n\nmodule counter_5to3(\n    input logic x1,x2,x3,x4,cin,\n    output logic sum,carry,cout\n);\n    assign sum = x1 ^ x2 ^ x3 ^ x4 ^ cin;\n    assign cout = (x1 ^ x2) & x3 | ~(x1 ^ x2) & x1;\n    assign carry = (x1 ^ x2 ^ x3 ^ x4) & cin | ~(x1 ^ x2 ^ x3 ^ x4) & x4;\nendmodule\n\nmodule compressor_3to2 #(\n    parameter int unsigned WIDTH_I = 8,\n    parameter int unsigned WIDTH_O = WIDTH_I + pdpu_pkg::clog2(3)\n)(\n    input logic [3*WIDTH_I-1:0] operands_i,\n    output logic [WIDTH_O-1:0] sum_o,\n    output logic [WIDTH_O-1:0] carry_o\n);\n    logic [WIDTH_I-1:0] sum;\n    logic [WIDTH_I-1:0] carry;\n    generate\n        genvar i;\n        for(i=0;i<WIDTH_I;i++) begin\n            fulladder u_fulladder(\n                .x(operands_i[0*WIDTH_I + i]),\n                .y(operands_i[1*WIDTH_I + i]),\n                .z(operands_i[2*WIDTH_I + i]),\n                .sum(sum[i]),\n                .carry(carry[i])\n            );\n        end\n    endgenerate\n\n    assign sum_o = sum;\n    assign carry_o = {1'b0,carry,1'b0};\nendmodule\n\nmodule compressor_4to2 #(\n    parameter int unsigned WIDTH_I = 8,\n    parameter int unsigned WIDTH_O = WIDTH_I + pdpu_pkg::clog2(4)\n)(\n    input logic [4*WIDTH_I-1:0] operands_i,\n    output logic [WIDTH_O-1:0] sum_o,\n    output logic [WIDTH_O-1:0] carry_o\n);\n    logic [WIDTH_I-1:0] sum;\n    logic [WIDTH_I:0] cin;\n    logic [WIDTH_I-1:0] cout;\n    logic [WIDTH_I-1:0] carry;\n\n    assign cin[0] = 1'b0;\n\n    generate\n        genvar i;\n        for(i=0;i<WIDTH_I;i++) begin\n            counter_5to3 u_counter_5to3(\n                .x1(operands_i[0*WIDTH_I + i]),\n                .x2(operands_i[1*WIDTH_I + i]),\n                .x3(operands_i[2*WIDTH_I + i]),\n                .x4(operands_i[3*WIDTH_I + i]),\n                .cin(cin[i]),\n                .sum(sum[i]),\n                .carry(carry[i]),\n                .cout(cout[i])\n            );\n            assign cin[i+1] = cout[i];\n        end\n    endgenerate\n\n    logic [1:0] carry_temp;\n\n    assign sum_o = sum;\n    assign carry_temp = carry[WIDTH_I-1]+cin[WIDTH_I];\n    assign carry_o = {carry_temp,carry[WIDTH_I-2:0],1'b0};\nendmodule\n\nmodule csa_tree #(\n    parameter int unsigned N = 8,\n    parameter int unsigned WIDTH_I = 8,\n    parameter int unsigned WIDTH_O = WIDTH_I + pdpu_pkg::clog2(N)\n)(\n    input logic [N*WIDTH_I-1:0] operands_i,\n    output logic [WIDTH_O-1:0] sum_o,\n    output logic [WIDTH_O-1:0] carry_o\n);\n    localparam int unsigned N_A = N/2;\n    localparam int unsigned N_B = N - N_A;\n\n    generate\n        if (N==1) begin\n            assign sum_o = operands_i[0 +: WIDTH_I];\n            assign carry_o = '0;\n        end\n        else if(N==2) begin\n            assign sum_o = operands_i[0 +: WIDTH_I];\n            assign carry_o = operands_i[WIDTH_I +: WIDTH_I];\n        end\n        else if(N==3) begin\n            compressor_3to2 #(\n                .WIDTH_I(WIDTH_I),\n                .WIDTH_O(WIDTH_O)\n            ) u_compressor_3to2 (\n                .operands_i(operands_i),\n                .sum_o(sum_o),\n                .carry_o(carry_o)\n            );\n        end\n        else if(N==4) begin\n            compressor_4to2 #(\n                .WIDTH_I(WIDTH_I),\n                .WIDTH_O(WIDTH_O)\n            ) u_compressor_4to2 (\n                .operands_i(operands_i),\n                .sum_o(sum_o),\n                .carry_o(carry_o)\n            );\n        end\n        else begin\n            logic [N_A*WIDTH_I-1:0] operands_i_A;\n            logic [N_B*WIDTH_I-1:0] operands_i_B;\n            logic [WIDTH_O-1:0] sum_o_A;\n            logic [WIDTH_O-1:0] sum_o_B;\n            logic [WIDTH_O-1:0] carry_o_A;\n            logic [WIDTH_O-1:0] carry_o_B;\n\n            assign operands_i_A = operands_i[0           +: N_A*WIDTH_I];\n            assign operands_i_B = operands_i[N_A*WIDTH_I +: N_B*WIDTH_I];\n\n            csa_tree #(\n                .N(N_A),\n                .WIDTH_I(WIDTH_I),\n                .WIDTH_O(WIDTH_O)\n            ) ua_csa_tree (\n                .operands_i(operands_i_A),\n                .sum_o(sum_o_A),\n                .carry_o(carry_o_A)\n            );\n\n            csa_tree #(\n                .N(N_B),\n                .WIDTH_I(WIDTH_I),\n                .WIDTH_O(WIDTH_O)\n            ) ub_csa_tree (\n                .operands_i(operands_i_B),\n                .sum_o(sum_o_B),\n                .carry_o(carry_o_B)\n            );\n\n            logic [4*WIDTH_O-1:0] operands_i_C;\n            assign operands_i_C = {sum_o_A, carry_o_A, sum_o_B, carry_o_B};\n\n            compressor_4to2 #(\n                .WIDTH_I(WIDTH_O),\n                .WIDTH_O(WIDTH_O)\n            ) uc_compressor_4to2 (\n                .operands_i(operands_i_C),\n                .sum_o(sum_o),\n                .carry_o(carry_o)\n            );\n        end\n    endgenerate\nendmodule\n\nmodule comparator #(\n    parameter int unsigned WIDTH = 8\n)(\n    input logic signed [WIDTH:0] operand_a,\n    input logic signed [WIDTH:0] operand_b,\n    output logic signed [WIDTH:0] result_o\n);\n    logic [1:0] sign_ab;\n    logic [WIDTH-1:0] data_a;\n    logic [WIDTH-1:0] data_b;\n\n    assign sign_ab = {operand_a[WIDTH],operand_b[WIDTH]};\n    assign data_a = operand_a[WIDTH-1:0];\n    assign data_b = operand_b[WIDTH-1:0];\n\n    always_comb begin\n        case(sign_ab)\n            2'b10: result_o = operand_b;\n            2'b01: result_o = operand_a;\n            default: result_o = (data_a>data_b) ? operand_a : operand_b;\n        endcase\n    end\nendmodule\n\nmodule comp_tree #(\n    parameter N = 4,\n    parameter WIDTH = 8\n)(\n    input logic signed [N*(WIDTH+1)-1:0] operands_i,\n    output logic signed [WIDTH:0] result_o\n);\n    localparam int unsigned W   = WIDTH + 1;\n    localparam int unsigned N_A = N/2;\n    localparam int unsigned N_B = N - N_A;\n\n    generate\n        if (N==1) begin : g_leaf\n            assign result_o = operands_i[0 +: W];\n        end\n        else if (N==2) begin : g_pair\n            comparator #(\n                .WIDTH(WIDTH)\n            ) u_comparator (\n                .operand_a(operands_i[0 +: W]),\n                .operand_b(operands_i[W +: W]),\n                .result_o(result_o)\n            );\n        end\n        else begin : g_split\n            logic signed [N_A*W-1:0] operands_i_A;\n            logic signed [N_B*W-1:0] operands_i_B;\n            logic signed [WIDTH:0]   result_o_A;\n            logic signed [WIDTH:0]   result_o_B;\n\n            assign operands_i_A = operands_i[0       +: N_A*W];\n            assign operands_i_B = operands_i[N_A*W   +: N_B*W];\n\n            comp_tree #(\n                .N(N_A),\n                .WIDTH(WIDTH)\n            ) ua_comp_tree (\n                .operands_i(operands_i_A),\n                .result_o(result_o_A)\n            );\n\n            comp_tree #(\n                .N(N_B),\n                .WIDTH(WIDTH)\n            ) ub_comp_tree (\n                .operands_i(operands_i_B),\n                .result_o(result_o_B)\n            );\n\n            comparator #(\n                .WIDTH(WIDTH)\n            ) uc_comparator (\n                .operand_a(result_o_A),\n                .operand_b(result_o_B),\n                .result_o(result_o)\n            );\n        end\n    endgenerate\nendmodule\n\nmodule barrel_shifter #(\n    parameter int unsigned WIDTH = 8,\n    parameter int unsigned SHIFT_WIDTH = 3,\n    parameter bit MODE = 1'b0\n)(\n    input logic [WIDTH-1:0] operand_i,\n    input logic [SHIFT_WIDTH-1:0] shift_amount,\n    output logic [WIDTH-1:0] result_o\n);\n    logic [SHIFT_WIDTH-1:0][WIDTH-1:0] temp_results;\n\n    assign temp_results[SHIFT_WIDTH-1] = operand_i;\n\n    generate\n        genvar i;\n        if(MODE==1'b0) begin\n            for(i=SHIFT_WIDTH-1;i>0;i--) begin\n                assign temp_results[i-1] = shift_amount[i] ? temp_results[i]<<(2**i) : temp_results[i];\n            end\n            assign result_o = shift_amount[0] ? temp_results[0]<<1 : temp_results;\n        end\n        else begin\n            for(i=SHIFT_WIDTH-1;i>0;i--) begin\n                assign temp_results[i-1] = shift_amount[i] ? temp_results[i]>>(2**i) : temp_results[i];\n            end\n            assign result_o = shift_amount[0] ? temp_results[0]>>1 : temp_results;\n        end\n    endgenerate\nendmodule\n\nmodule lzc #(\n  parameter int unsigned WIDTH = 2,\n  parameter bit          MODE  = 1'b0,\n  parameter int unsigned CNT_WIDTH = cf_math_pkg::idx_width(WIDTH)\n) (\n  input  logic [WIDTH-1:0]     in_i,\n  output logic [CNT_WIDTH-1:0] cnt_o,\n  output logic                 empty_o\n);\n\n  if (WIDTH == 1) begin : gen_degenerate_lzc\n\n    assign cnt_o[0] = !in_i[0];\n    assign empty_o = !in_i[0];\n\n  end else begin : gen_lzc\n\n    localparam int unsigned NumLevels = cf_math_pkg::clog(WIDTH);\n\n    logic [WIDTH*NumLevels-1:0] index_lut;\n    logic [2**NumLevels-1:0] sel_nodes;\n    logic [(2**NumLevels)*NumLevels-1:0] index_nodes;\n\n    logic [WIDTH-1:0] in_tmp;\n\n    always_comb begin : flip_vector\n      for (int unsigned i = 0; i < WIDTH; i++) begin\n        in_tmp[i] = (MODE) ? in_i[WIDTH-1-i] : in_i[i];\n      end\n    end\n\n    for (genvar j = 0; $unsigned(j) < WIDTH; j++) begin : g_index_lut\n      assign index_lut[(j)*NumLevels +: NumLevels] = ($unsigned(j));\n    end\n\n    for (genvar level = 0; $unsigned(level) < NumLevels; level++) begin : g_levels\n      if ($unsigned(level) == NumLevels - 1) begin : g_last_level\n        for (genvar k = 0; k < 2 ** level; k++) begin : g_level\n          if ($unsigned(k) * 2 < WIDTH - 1) begin : g_reduce\n            assign sel_nodes[2 ** level - 1 + k] = in_tmp[k * 2] | in_tmp[k * 2 + 1];\n            assign index_nodes[(2 ** level - 1 + k)*NumLevels +: NumLevels] = (in_tmp[k * 2] == 1'b1)\n              ? index_lut[(k * 2)*NumLevels +: NumLevels] :\n                index_lut[(k * 2 + 1)*NumLevels +: NumLevels];\n          end\n          if ($unsigned(k) * 2 == WIDTH - 1) begin : g_base\n            assign sel_nodes[2 ** level - 1 + k] = in_tmp[k * 2];\n            assign index_nodes[(2 ** level - 1 + k)*NumLevels +: NumLevels] = index_lut[(k * 2)*NumLevels +: NumLevels];\n          end\n          if ($unsigned(k) * 2 > WIDTH - 1) begin : g_out_of_range\n            assign sel_nodes[2 ** level - 1 + k] = 1'b0;\n            assign index_nodes[(2 ** level - 1 + k)*NumLevels +: NumLevels] = '0;\n          end\n        end\n      end else begin : g_not_last_level\n        for (genvar l = 0; l < 2 ** level; l++) begin : g_level\n          assign sel_nodes[2 ** level - 1 + l] =\n              sel_nodes[2 ** (level + 1) - 1 + l * 2] | sel_nodes[2 ** (level + 1) - 1 + l * 2 + 1];\n          assign index_nodes[(2 ** level - 1 + l)*NumLevels +: NumLevels] = (sel_nodes[2 ** (level + 1) - 1 + l * 2] == 1'b1)\n            ? index_nodes[(2 ** (level + 1) - 1 + l * 2)*NumLevels +: NumLevels] :\n              index_nodes[(2 ** (level + 1) - 1 + l * 2 + 1)*NumLevels +: NumLevels];\n        end\n      end\n    end\n\n    assign cnt_o = NumLevels > $unsigned(0) ? index_nodes[(0)*NumLevels +: NumLevels] : {(cf_math_pkg::clog(WIDTH)) {1'b0}};\n    assign empty_o = NumLevels > $unsigned(0) ? ~sel_nodes[0] : ~(|in_i);\n\n  end : gen_lzc\n\nendmodule : lzc\n\nmodule mantissa_norm #(\n    parameter int unsigned WIDTH = 8,\n    parameter int unsigned EXP_WIDTH = 3,\n    parameter int unsigned DECIMAL_POINT = 3\n)(\n    input logic [WIDTH-1:0] operand_i,\n    output logic signed [EXP_WIDTH:0] exp_adjust,\n    output logic [WIDTH-1:0] result_o\n);\n    localparam int unsigned LZC_WIDTH = pdpu_pkg::clog2(WIDTH);\n    logic [LZC_WIDTH-1:0] leading_zero_count;\n    logic lzc_zeroes;\n\n    lzc #(\n        .WIDTH(WIDTH),\n        .MODE(1'b1)\n    ) u_lzc (\n        .in_i(operand_i),\n        .cnt_o(leading_zero_count),\n        .empty_o(lzc_zeroes)\n    );\n\n    always_comb begin\n        if(lzc_zeroes) begin\n            exp_adjust = '0;\n        end\n        else if(leading_zero_count <= DECIMAL_POINT - 1) begin\n            exp_adjust = DECIMAL_POINT - leading_zero_count - 1;\n        end\n        else begin\n            exp_adjust = -$signed(leading_zero_count - DECIMAL_POINT + 1);\n        end\n    end\n\n    barrel_shifter #(\n        .WIDTH(WIDTH),\n        .SHIFT_WIDTH(LZC_WIDTH),\n        .MODE(1'b0)\n    ) u_barrel_shifter (\n        .operand_i(operand_i),\n        .shift_amount(leading_zero_count),\n        .result_o(result_o)\n    );\nendmodule\n\nmodule posit_decoder #(\n    parameter int unsigned n = 16,\n    parameter int unsigned es = 1,\n    parameter int unsigned nd = pdpu_pkg::clog2(n-1),\n    parameter int unsigned EXP_WIDTH = nd+es,\n    parameter int unsigned MANT_WIDTH = n-es-3\n)(\n    input logic [n-1:0] operand_i,\n    output logic sign_o,\n    output logic signed [EXP_WIDTH:0] rg_exp_o,\n    output logic [MANT_WIDTH:0] mant_norm_o\n);\n\n    logic sign;\n    logic [n-2:0] operand_value;\n\n    assign sign = operand_i[n-1];\n    assign operand_value = sign ? (~operand_i[n-2:0]+1) : operand_i[n-2:0];\n\n    logic regS;\n    logic [n-2:0] lzc_operand;\n    logic [nd-1:0] leading_zero_count;\n    logic lzc_zeroes;\n\n    assign regS = operand_value[n-2];\n    assign lzc_operand = regS ? (~operand_value) : operand_value;\n\n    lzc #(\n        .WIDTH(n-1),\n        .MODE(1'b1)\n    ) u_lzc (\n        .in_i(lzc_operand),\n        .cnt_o(leading_zero_count),\n        .empty_o(lzc_zeroes)\n    );\n\n    logic [nd-1:0] runlength;\n    logic [nd-1:0] regime_bits;\n    logic signed [nd:0] regime_k;\n\n    assign runlength = lzc_zeroes ? (n-1) : leading_zero_count;\n    assign regime_bits = lzc_zeroes ? (n-1) : (leading_zero_count+1);\n    assign regime_k = regS ? ({1'b0,runlength-1}) : ({1'b1,~runlength+1});\n\n    logic [n-2:0] op_no_rg;\n\n    barrel_shifter #(\n        .WIDTH(n-1),\n        .SHIFT_WIDTH(nd),\n        .MODE(1'b0)\n    ) u_barrel_shifter (\n        .operand_i(operand_value),\n        .shift_amount(regime_bits),\n        .result_o(op_no_rg)\n    );\n\n    assign sign_o = sign;\n\n    logic [es:0] exp;\n    if(es==0) begin\n        assign exp = 0;\n    end\n    else begin\n        assign exp = op_no_rg[n-2:n-2-es+1];\n    end\n\n    assign rg_exp_o = regime_k << es | exp;\n\n    logic implicit_bit;\n\n    assign implicit_bit = |operand_i[n-2:0];\n    assign mant_norm_o = {implicit_bit, op_no_rg[n-2-es:2]};\nendmodule\n\nmodule posit_encoder #(\n    parameter int unsigned n = 16,\n    parameter int unsigned es = 1,\n    parameter int unsigned nd = pdpu_pkg::clog2(n-1),\n    parameter int unsigned EXP_WIDTH = nd+es,\n    parameter int unsigned MANT_WIDTH = n-es-3\n) (\n    input logic sign_i,\n    input logic signed [EXP_WIDTH:0] rg_exp_i,\n    input logic [MANT_WIDTH:0] mant_norm_i,\n\n    output logic [n-1:0] result_o\n);\n    logic input_not_zero;\n    assign input_not_zero = mant_norm_i[MANT_WIDTH];\n\n    logic signed [EXP_WIDTH-es:0] regime_k;\n    logic signed [es:0] exp;\n\n    assign regime_k = rg_exp_i[EXP_WIDTH:es];\n    if(es==0) begin\n        assign exp = 0;\n    end\n    else begin\n        assign exp = rg_exp_i[es-1:0];\n    end\n\n    logic sign_k;\n    logic [n-2:0] rg_const;\n    logic [n-2:0] regime;\n\n    assign sign_k = rg_exp_i[EXP_WIDTH];\n    assign rg_const = 1;\n    assign regime = sign_k ? rg_const : ~rg_const;\n\n    logic [EXP_WIDTH-es:0] regime_bits;\n\n    assign regime_bits = sign_k ? (~regime_k+2) : (regime_k+2);\n\n    localparam int unsigned TEMP_WIDTH = (n-1)+es+MANT_WIDTH;\n    logic [TEMP_WIDTH-1:0] rg_exp_mant;\n\n    if(es==0) begin\n        assign rg_exp_mant = {regime, mant_norm_i[MANT_WIDTH-1:0]};\n    end\n    else begin\n        assign rg_exp_mant = {regime, exp[es-1:0], mant_norm_i[MANT_WIDTH-1:0]};\n    end\n\n    localparam int unsigned MAX_SHIFT_AMOUNT = MANT_WIDTH + es + 1;\n    localparam int unsigned SHIFT_WIDTH = pdpu_pkg::clog2(MAX_SHIFT_AMOUNT+1);\n    logic [SHIFT_WIDTH-1:0] shift_amount;\n\n    assign shift_amount = (regime_bits>=n) ? MAX_SHIFT_AMOUNT : (regime_bits+(MANT_WIDTH+es-n+1));\n\n    logic [TEMP_WIDTH+MAX_SHIFT_AMOUNT-1:0] value_before_shift, value_after_shift;\n\n    assign value_before_shift = rg_exp_mant << MAX_SHIFT_AMOUNT;\n    barrel_shifter #(\n        .WIDTH(TEMP_WIDTH+MAX_SHIFT_AMOUNT),\n        .SHIFT_WIDTH(SHIFT_WIDTH),\n        .MODE(1'b1)\n    ) u_barrel_shifter (\n        .operand_i(value_before_shift),\n        .shift_amount(shift_amount),\n        .result_o(value_after_shift)\n    );\n\n    logic [n-2:0] value_before_round;\n    logic [MAX_SHIFT_AMOUNT-1:0] rounding_bits;\n\n    assign {value_before_round,rounding_bits} = value_after_shift[MAX_SHIFT_AMOUNT+n-2:0];\n\n    logic round_bit;\n    logic sticky_bit;\n    logic round_value;\n    logic [n-2:0] value_after_round;\n\n    assign round_bit = rounding_bits[MAX_SHIFT_AMOUNT-1];\n    assign sticky_bit = |rounding_bits[MAX_SHIFT_AMOUNT-2:0];\n    assign round_value = round_bit & (sticky_bit | value_before_round[0]);\n    assign value_after_round = value_before_round + round_value;\n\n    logic [n-1:0] normal_result;\n    assign normal_result = sign_i ? {1'b1,~value_after_round+1} : {1'b0,value_after_round};\n    assign result_o = input_not_zero ? normal_result : '0;\n\nendmodule\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Pipelined top\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\nmodule pdpu_top_pipelined #(\n    parameter int unsigned N = 16,\n    parameter int unsigned n_i = 8,\n    parameter int unsigned es_i = 2,\n    parameter int unsigned n_o = 16,\n    parameter int unsigned es_o = 2,\n    parameter int unsigned ALIGN_WIDTH = 14,\n    parameter int unsigned CARRY_W = 5,\n    parameter int unsigned SUM_W = 19\n)(\n    input logic clk,\n    input logic [N*n_i-1:0] operands_a,\n    input logic [N*n_i-1:0] operands_b,\n    input logic [n_o-1:0] acc,\n    output logic [n_o-1:0] result_o\n);\n    localparam int unsigned EXP_WIDTH_I = pdpu_pkg::clog2(n_i-1) + es_i;   // 5\n    localparam int unsigned MANT_WIDTH_I = n_i-es_i-3;                     // 3\n    localparam int unsigned EXP_WIDTH_O = pdpu_pkg::clog2(n_o-1) + es_o;   // 6\n    localparam int unsigned MANT_WIDTH_O = n_o-es_o-3;                     // 11\n    localparam int unsigned EXP_WIDTH = pdpu_pkg::maximum(EXP_WIDTH_I+1,EXP_WIDTH_O); // 6\n    localparam int unsigned MULW = 2*(MANT_WIDTH_I+1);                     // 8\n    localparam int unsigned SUM_WIDTH = ALIGN_WIDTH + CARRY_W;             // 19\n    localparam int unsigned SHIFT_WIDTH = pdpu_pkg::clog2(ALIGN_WIDTH+1);  // 4\n    localparam int unsigned LANE_W = SUM_WIDTH;                            // 19\n\n    // ================= Stage 1: decode + exponent adds + popcount =================\n    logic [N-1:0] signs_a, signs_b;\n    logic signed [N*(EXP_WIDTH_I+1)-1:0] rg_exp_a, rg_exp_b;\n    logic [N*(MANT_WIDTH_I+1)-1:0] mants_a, mants_b;\n\n    generate\n        genvar i;\n        for(i=0;i<N;i++) begin: posit_decoding\n            posit_decoder #(.n(n_i),.es(es_i)) ua_posit_decoder (\n                .operand_i(operands_a[(i)*n_i +: n_i]),\n                .sign_o(signs_a[i]),\n                .rg_exp_o(rg_exp_a[(i)*(EXP_WIDTH_I+1) +: (EXP_WIDTH_I+1)]),\n                .mant_norm_o(mants_a[(i)*(MANT_WIDTH_I+1) +: (MANT_WIDTH_I+1)])\n            );\n            posit_decoder #(.n(n_i),.es(es_i)) ub_posit_decoder (\n                .operand_i(operands_b[(i)*n_i +: n_i]),\n                .sign_o(signs_b[i]),\n                .rg_exp_o(rg_exp_b[(i)*(EXP_WIDTH_I+1) +: (EXP_WIDTH_I+1)]),\n                .mant_norm_o(mants_b[(i)*(MANT_WIDTH_I+1) +: (MANT_WIDTH_I+1)])\n            );\n        end\n    endgenerate\n\n    logic sign_acc;\n    logic signed [EXP_WIDTH_O:0] rg_exp_acc;\n    logic [MANT_WIDTH_O:0] mant_norm_acc;\n\n    posit_decoder #(.n(n_o),.es(es_o)) uc_posit_decoder (\n        .operand_i(acc),\n        .sign_o(sign_acc),\n        .rg_exp_o(rg_exp_acc),\n        .mant_norm_o(mant_norm_acc)\n    );\n\n    logic [N-1:0] signs_ab;\n    logic [N:0] s1_signs;\n    assign signs_ab = signs_a ^ signs_b;\n    assign s1_signs = {sign_acc, signs_ab};\n\n    logic signed [N*(EXP_WIDTH+1)-1:0] s1_rg_exp_c;\n    generate\n        genvar m;\n        for(m=0;m<N;m++) begin\n            assign s1_rg_exp_c[(m)*(EXP_WIDTH+1) +: (EXP_WIDTH+1)] =\n                $signed(rg_exp_a[(m)*(EXP_WIDTH_I+1) +: (EXP_WIDTH_I+1)]) +\n                $signed(rg_exp_b[(m)*(EXP_WIDTH_I+1) +: (EXP_WIDTH_I+1)]);\n        end\n    endgenerate\n\n    // balanced popcount of signs (two's complement correction term)\n    logic [15:0] pc1;\n    logic [11:0] pc2;\n    logic [7:0]  pc3;\n    logic [4:0] s1_corr;\n    generate\n        genvar p;\n        for(p=0;p<8;p++) begin\n            assign pc1[2*p +: 2] = {1'b0, s1_signs[2*p]} + {1'b0, s1_signs[2*p+1]};\n        end\n        for(p=0;p<4;p++) begin\n            assign pc2[3*p +: 3] = {1'b0, pc1[4*p +: 2]} + {1'b0, pc1[4*p+2 +: 2]};\n        end\n        for(p=0;p<2;p++) begin\n            assign pc3[4*p +: 4] = {1'b0, pc2[6*p +: 3]} + {1'b0, pc2[6*p+3 +: 3]};\n        end\n    endgenerate\n    assign s1_corr = {1'b0, pc3[3:0]} + {1'b0, pc3[7:4]} + {4'b0, s1_signs[N]};\n\n    // ---- pipe1 ----\n    logic [N:0] pipe1_signs;\n    logic signed [N*(EXP_WIDTH+1)-1:0] pipe1_rg_exp_c;\n    logic [N*(MANT_WIDTH_I+1)-1:0] pipe1_mants_norm_a, pipe1_mants_norm_b;\n    logic signed [EXP_WIDTH_O:0] pipe1_rg_exp_acc;\n    logic [MANT_WIDTH_O:0] pipe1_mant_norm_acc;\n    logic [4:0] pipe1_corr;\n\n    always_ff @(posedge clk) begin\n        pipe1_signs         <= s1_signs;\n        pipe1_rg_exp_c      <= s1_rg_exp_c;\n        pipe1_mants_norm_a  <= mants_a;\n        pipe1_mants_norm_b  <= mants_b;\n        pipe1_rg_exp_acc    <= rg_exp_acc;\n        pipe1_mant_norm_acc <= mant_norm_acc;\n        pipe1_corr          <= s1_corr;\n    end\n\n    // ============ Stage 2: multiplies + max tree ============\n    logic [N*MULW-1:0] s2_prod;\n    generate\n        genvar j;\n        for(j=0;j<N;j++) begin\n            assign s2_prod[(j)*MULW +: MULW] =\n                pipe1_mants_norm_a[(j)*(MANT_WIDTH_I+1) +: (MANT_WIDTH_I+1)] *\n                pipe1_mants_norm_b[(j)*(MANT_WIDTH_I+1) +: (MANT_WIDTH_I+1)];\n        end\n    endgenerate\n\n    logic signed [(N+1)*(EXP_WIDTH+1)-1:0] rg_exp_items;\n    generate\n        genvar u;\n        for(u=0;u<N;u++) begin\n            assign rg_exp_items[(u)*(EXP_WIDTH+1) +: (EXP_WIDTH+1)] =\n                pipe1_rg_exp_c[(u)*(EXP_WIDTH+1) +: (EXP_WIDTH+1)];\n        end\n    endgenerate\n    assign rg_exp_items[(N)*(EXP_WIDTH+1) +: (EXP_WIDTH+1)] = $signed(pipe1_rg_exp_acc);\n\n    logic signed [EXP_WIDTH:0] rg_exp_max;\n    comp_tree #(\n        .N(N+1),\n        .WIDTH(EXP_WIDTH)\n    ) u_comp_tree (\n        .operands_i(rg_exp_items),\n        .result_o(rg_exp_max)\n    );\n\n    // ---- pipe2 ----\n    logic [N:0] pipe2_signs;\n    logic signed [(N+1)*(EXP_WIDTH+1)-1:0] @(posedge clk) begin\n        pipe2_signs         <= pipe1_signs;\n        pipe2_rg_exp_items  <= rg_exp_items;\n        pipe2_rg_exp_max    <= rg_exp_max;\n        pipe2_prod          <= s2_prod;\n        pipe2_mant_norm_acc <= pipe1_mant_norm_acc;\n        pipe2_corr          <= pipe1_corr;\n    end\n\n    // ============ Stage 3: subtract + align + sign-XOR ============\n    logic (|rg_exp_diff[(EXP_WIDTH+1)-1:SHIFT_WIDTH]) ? ALIGN_WIDTH[SHIFT_WIDTH-1:0]\n                                                                                    : rg_exp_diff[SHIFT_WIDTH-1:0];\n            end\n            else begin\n shifted ^ {ALIGN_WIDTH{pipe2_signs[s]}};\n        end\n    endgenerate\n\n    // ---- pipe3 ----\n    logic [N:0] pipe3_signs;\n    logic [(N+1)*ALIGN_WIDTH-1:0] pipe3_shifted_xor;\n    logic signed [EXP_WIDTH:0] pipe3_rg_exp_max;\n    logic [4:0] pipe3_corr;\n\n    always_ff @(posedge clk) begin\n        pipe3_signs         <= pipe2_signs;\n        pipe3_shifted_xor   <= s3_shifted_xor;\n        pipe3_rg_exp_max    <= pipe2_rg_exp_max;\n        pipe3_corr          <= pipe2_corr;\n    end\n\n    // ============ Stage 4: sign-extend, CSA tree, 19-bit CPA ============\n    logic [EXP_WIDTH:0] pipe4_rg_exp_max;\n\n    always_ff @(posedge clk) begin\n        pipe4_sum_result <= sum_result;\n        pipe4_rg_exp_max <= pipe3_rg_exp_max;\n    end\n\n    // ============ Stage 5:
