endgenerate\nendmodule\n\nmodule lzc #(\n  parameter int unsigned WIDTH = 2,\n  parameter bit          MODE  = 1'b0,\n  parameter int unsigned CNT_WIDTH = cf_math_pkg::idx_width(WIDTH)\n) (\n  input  logic [WIDTH-1:0]     in_i,\n  output logic [CNT_WIDTH-1:0] cnt_o,\n  output logic                 empty_o\n);\n\n  if (WIDTH == 1) begin : gen_degenerate_lzc\n\n    assign cnt_o[0] = !in_i[0];\n    assign empty_o = !in_i[0];\n\n  end else begin : gen_lzc\n\n    localparam int unsigned NumLevels = cf_math_pkg::clog(WIDTH);\n\n    logic [WIDTH*NumLevels-1:0] index_lut;\n    logic [2**NumLevels-1:0] sel_nodes;\n    logic [(2**NumLevels)*NumLevels-1:0] index_nodes;\n\n    logic [WIDTH-1:0] in_tmp;\n\n    always_comb begin : flip_vector\n      for (int unsigned i = 0; i < WIDTH; i++) begin\n        in_tmp[i] = (MODE) ? in_i[WIDTH-1-i] : in_i[i];\n      end\n    end\n\n    for (genvar j = 0; $unsigned(j) < WIDTH; j++) begin : g_index_lut\n      assign index_lut[(j)*NumLevels +: NumLevels] = ($unsigned(j));\n    end\n\n    for (genvar level = 0; $unsigned(level) < NumLevels; level++) begin : g_levels\n      if ($unsigned(level) == NumLevels - 1) begin : g_last_level\n        for (genvar k = 0; k < 2 ** level; k++) begin : g_level\n          if ($unsigned(k) * 2 < WIDTH - 1) begin : g_reduce\n            assign sel_nodes[2 ** level - 1 + k] = in_tmp[k * 2] | in_tmp[k * 2 + 1];\n            assign index_nodes[(2 ** level - 1 + k)*NumLevels +: NumLevels] = (in_tmp[k * 2] == 1'b1)\n              ? index_lut[(k * 2)*NumLevels +: NumLevels] :\n                index_lut[(k * 2 + 1)*NumLevels +: NumLevels];\n          end\n          if ($unsigned(k) * 2 == WIDTH - 1) begin : g_base\n            assign sel_nodes[2 ** level - 1 + k] = in_tmp[k * 2];\n            assign index_nodes[(2 ** level - 1 + k)*NumLevels +: NumLevels] = index_lut[(k * 2)*NumLevels +: NumLevels];\n          end\n          if ($unsigned(k) * 2 > WIDTH - 1) begin : g_out_of_range\n            assign sel_nodes[2 ** level - 1 + k] = 1'b0;\n            assign index_nodes[(2 ** level - 1 + k)*NumLevels +: NumLevels] = '0;\n          end\n        end\n      end else begin : g_not_last_level\n        for (genvar l = 0; l < 2 ** level; l++) begin : g_level\n          assign sel_nodes[2 ** level - 1 + l] =\n              sel_nodes[2 ** (level + 1) - 1 + l * 2] | sel_nodes[2 ** (level + 1) - 1 + l * 2 + 1];\n          assign index_nodes[(2 ** level - 1 + l)*NumLevels +: NumLevels] = (sel_nodes[2 ** (level + 1) - 1 + l * 2] == 1'b1)\n            ? index_nodes[(2 ** (level + 1) - 1 + l * 2)*NumLevels +: NumLevels] :\n              index_nodes[(2 ** (level + 1) - 1 + l * 2 + 1)*NumLevels +: NumLevels];\n        end\n      end\n    end\n\n    assign cnt_o = NumLevels > $unsigned(0) ? index_nodes[(0)*NumLevels +: NumLevels] : {(cf_math_pkg::clog(WIDTH)) {1'b0}};\n    assign empty_o = NumLevels > $unsigned(0) ? ~sel_nodes[0] : ~(|in_i);\n\n  end : gen_lzc\n\nendmodule : lzc\n\nmodule mantissa_norm #(\n    parameter int unsigned WIDTH = 8,\n    parameter int unsigned EXP_WIDTH = 3,\n    parameter int unsigned DECIMAL_POINT = 3\n)(\n    input logic [WIDTH-1:0] operand_i,\n    output logic signed [EXP_WIDTH:0] exp_adjust,\n    output logic [WIDTH-1:0] result_o\n);\n    localparam int unsigned LZC_WIDTH = pdpu_pkg::clog2(WIDTH);\n    logic [LZC_WIDTH-1:0] leading_zero_count;\n    logic lzc_zeroes;\n\n    lzc #(\n        .WIDTH(WIDTH),\n        .MODE(1'b1)\n    ) u_lzc (\n        .in_i(operand_i),\n        .cnt_o(leading_zero_count),\n        .empty_o(lzc_zeroes)\n    );\n\n    always_comb begin\n        if(lzc_zeroes) begin\n            exp_adjust = '0;\n        end\n        else if(leading_zero_count <= DECIMAL_POINT - 1) begin\n            exp_adjust = DECIMAL_POINT - leading_zero_count - 1;\n        end\n        else begin\n            exp_adjust = -$signed(leading_zero_count - DECIMAL_POINT + 1);\n        end\n    end\n\n    barrel_shifter #(\n        .WIDTH(WIDTH),\n        .SHIFT_WIDTH(LZC_WIDTH),\n        .MODE(1'b0)\n    ) u_barrel_shifter (\n        .operand_i(operand_i),\n        .shift_amount(leading_zero_count),\n        .result_o(result_o)\n    );\nendmodule\n\nmodule posit_decoder #(\n    parameter int unsigned n = 16,\n    parameter int unsigned es = 1,\n    parameter int unsigned nd = pdpu_pkg::clog2(n-1),\n    parameter int unsigned EXP_WIDTH = nd+es,\n    parameter int unsigned MANT_WIDTH = n-es-3\n)(\n    input logic [n-1:0] operand_i,\n    output logic sign_o,\n    output logic signed [EXP_WIDTH:0] rg_exp_o,\n    output logic [MANT_WIDTH:0] mant_norm_o\n);\n\n    logic sign;\n    logic [n-2:0] operand_value;\n\n    assign sign = operand_i[n-1];\n    assign operand_value = sign ? (~operand_i[n-2:0]+1) : operand_i[n-2:0];\n\n    logic regS;\n    logic [n-2:0] lzc_operand;\n    logic [nd-1:0] leading_zero_count;\n    logic lzc_zeroes;\n\n    assign regS = operand_value[n-2];\n    assign lzc_operand = regS ? (~operand_value) : operand_value;\n\n    lzc #(\n        .WIDTH(n-1),\n        .MODE(1'b1)\n    ) u_lzc (\n        .in_i(lzc_operand),\n        .cnt_o(leading_zero_count),\n        .empty_o(lzc_zeroes)\n    );\n\n    logic [nd-1:0] runlength;\n    logic [nd-1:0] regime_bits;\n    logic signed [nd:0] regime_k;\n\n    assign runlength = lzc_zeroes ? (n-1) : leading_zero_count;\n    assign regime_bits = lzc_zeroes ? (n-1) : (leading_zero_count+1);\n    assign regime_k = regS ? ({1'b0,runlength-1}) : ({1'b1,~runlength+1});\n\n    logic [n-2:0] op_no_rg;\n\n    barrel_shifter #(\n        .WIDTH(n-1),\n        .SHIFT_WIDTH(nd),\n        .MODE(1'b0)\n    ) u_barrel_shifter (\n        .operand_i(operand_value),\n        .shift_amount(regime_bits),\n        .result_o(op_no_rg)\n    );\n\n    assign sign_o = sign;\n\n    logic [es:0] exp;\n    if(es==0) begin\n        assign exp = 0;\n    end\n    else begin\n        assign exp = op_no_rg[n-2:n-2-es+1];\n    end\n\n    assign rg_exp_o = regime_k << es | exp;\n\n    logic implicit_bit;\n\n    assign implicit_bit = |operand_i[n-2:0];\n    assign mant_norm_o = {implicit_bit, op_no_rg[n-2-es:2]};\nendmodule\n\nmodule posit_encoder #(\n    parameter int unsigned n = 16,\n    parameter int unsigned es = 1,\n    parameter int unsigned nd = pdpu_pkg::clog2(n-1),\n    parameter int unsigned EXP_WIDTH = nd+es,\n    parameter int unsigned MANT_WIDTH = n-es-3\n) (\n    input logic sign_i,\n    input logic signed [EXP_WIDTH:0] rg_exp_i,\n    input logic [MANT_WIDTH:0] mant_norm_i,\n\n    output logic [n-1:0] result_o\n);\n    logic input_not_zero;\n    assign input_not_zero = mant_norm_i[MANT_WIDTH];\n\n    logic signed [EXP_WIDTH-es:0] regime_k;\n    logic signed [es:0] exp;\n\n    assign regime_k = rg_exp_i[EXP_WIDTH:es];\n    if(es==0) begin\n        assign exp = 0;\n    end\n    else begin\n        assign exp = rg_exp_i[es-1:0];\n    end\n\n    logic sign_k;\n    logic [n-2:0] rg_const;\n    logic [n-2:0] regime;\n\n    assign sign_k = rg_exp_i[EXP_WIDTH];\n    assign rg_const = 1;\n    assign regime = sign_k ? rg_const : ~rg_const;\n\n    logic [EXP_WIDTH-es:0] regime_bits;\n\n    assign regime_bits = sign_k ? (~regime_k+2) : (regime_k+2);\n\n    localparam int unsigned TEMP_WIDTH = (n-1)+es+MANT_WIDTH;\n    logic [TEMP_WIDTH-1:0] rg_exp_mant;\n\n    if(es==0) begin\n        assign rg_exp_mant = {regime, mant_norm_i[MANT_WIDTH-1:0]};\n    end\n    else begin\n        assign rg_exp_mant = {regime, exp[es-1:0], mant_norm_i[MANT_WIDTH-1:0]};\n    end\n\n    localparam int unsigned MAX_SHIFT_AMOUNT = MANT_WIDTH + es + 1;\n    localparam int unsigned SHIFT_WIDTH = pdpu_pkg::clog2(MAX_SHIFT_AMOUNT+1);\n    logic [SHIFT_WIDTH-1:0] shift_amount;\n\n    assign shift_amount = (regime_bits>=n) ? MAX_SHIFT_AMOUNT : (regime_bits+(MANT_WIDTH+es-n+1));\n\n    logic [TEMP_WIDTH+MAX_SHIFT_AMOUNT-1:0] value_before_shift, value_after_shift;\n\n    assign value_before_shift = rg_exp_mant << MAX_SHIFT_AMOUNT;\n    barrel_shifter #(\n        .WIDTH(TEMP_WIDTH+MAX_SHIFT_AMOUNT),\n        .SHIFT_WIDTH(SHIFT_WIDTH),\n        .MODE(1'b1)\n    ) u_barrel_shifter (\n        .operand_i(value_before_shift),\n        .shift_amount(shift_amount),\n        .result_o(value_after_shift)\n    );\n\n    logic [n-2:0] value_before_round;\n    logic [MAX_SHIFT_AMOUNT-1:0] rounding_bits;\n\n    assign {value_before_round,rounding_bits} = value_after_shift[MAX_SHIFT_AMOUNT+n-2:0];\n\n    logic round_bit;\n    logic sticky_bit;\n    logic round_value;\n    logic [n-2:0] value_after_round;\n\n    assign round_bit = rounding_bits[MAX_SHIFT_AMOUNT-1];\n    assign sticky_bit = |rounding_bits[MAX_SHIFT_AMOUNT-2:0];\n    assign round_value = round_bit & (sticky_bit | value_before_round[0]);\n    assign value_after_round = value_before_round + round_value;\n\n    logic [n-1:0] normal_result;\n    assign normal_result = sign_i ? {1'b1,~value_after_round+1} : {1'b0,value_after_round};\n    assign result_o = input_not_zero ? normal_result : '0;\n\nendmodule\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Pipelined top\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\nmodule pdpu_top_pipelined #(\n    parameter int unsigned N = 16,\n    parameter int unsigned n_i = 8,\n    parameter int unsigned es_i = 2,\n    parameter int unsigned n_o = 16,\n    parameter int unsigned es_o = 2,\n    parameter int unsigned ALIGN_WIDTH = 14,\n    parameter int unsigned CARRY_W = 5,\n    parameter int unsigned SUM_W = 19\n)(\n    input logic clk,\n    input logic [N*n_i-1:0] operands_a,\n    input logic [N*n_i-1:0] operands_b,\n    input logic [n_o-1:0] acc,\n    output logic [n_o-1:0] result_o\n);\n    localparam int unsigned EXP_WIDTH_I = pdpu_pkg::clog2(n_i-1) + es_i;   // 5\n    localparam int unsigned MANT_WIDTH_I = n_i-es_i-3;                     // 3\n    localparam int unsigned EXP_WIDTH_O = pdpu_pkg::clog2(n_o-1) + es_o;   // 6\n    localparam int unsigned MANT_WIDTH_O = n_o-es_o-3;                     // 11\n    localparam int unsigned EXP_WIDTH = pdpu_pkg::maximum(EXP_WIDTH_I+1,EXP_WIDTH_O); // 6\n    localparam int unsigned MULW = 2*(MANT_WIDTH_I+1);                     // 8\n    localparam int unsigned SUM_WIDTH = ALIGN_WIDTH + CARRY_W;             // 19\n    localparam int unsigned SHIFT_WIDTH = pdpu_pkg::clog2(ALIGN_WIDTH+1);  // 4\n    localparam int unsigned LANE_W = SUM_WIDTH;                            // 19\n\n    // ================= Stage 1: decode, exp adds, pairwise max, popcount =================\n    logic [N-1:0] signs_a, signs_b;\n    logic signed [N*(EXP_WIDTH_I+1)-1:0] rg_exp_a, rg_exp_b;\n    logic [N*(MANT_WIDTH_I+1)-1:0] mants_a, mants_b;\n\n    generate\n        genvar i;\n        for(i=0;i<N;i++) begin: posit_decoding\n            posit_decoder #(.n(n_i),.es(es_i)) ua_posit_decoder (\n                .operand_i(operands_a[(i)*n_i +: n_i]),\n                .sign_o(signs_a[i]),\n                .rg_exp_o(rg_exp_a[(i)*(EXP_WIDTH_I+1) +: (EXP_WIDTH_I+1)]),\n                .mant_norm_o(mants_a[(i)*(MANT_WIDTH_I+1) +: (MANT_WIDTH_I+1)])\n            );\n            posit_decoder #(.n(n_i),.es(es_i)) ub_posit_decoder (\n                .operand_i(operands_b[(i)*n_i +: n_i]),\n                .sign_o(signs_b[i]),\n                .rg_exp_o(rg_exp_b[(i)*(EXP_WIDTH_I+1) +: (EXP_WIDTH_I+1)]),\n                .mant_norm_o(mants_b[(i)*(MANT_WIDTH_I+1) +: (MANT_WIDTH_I+1)])\n            );\n        end\n    endgenerate\n\n    logic sign_acc;\n    logic signed [EXP_WIDTH_O:0] rg_exp_acc;\n    logic [MANT_WIDTH_O:0] mant_norm_acc;\n\n    posit_decoder #(.n(n_o),.es(es_o)) uc_posit_decoder (\n        .operand_i(acc),\n        .sign_o(sign_acc),\n        .rg_exp_o(rg_exp_acc),\n        .mant_norm_o(mant_norm_acc)\n    );\n\n    logic [N-1:0] signs_ab;\n    logic [N:0] s1_signs;\n    assign signs_ab = signs_a ^ signs_b;\n    assign s1_signs = {sign_acc, signs_ab};\n\n    // exponent sums\n    logic signed [N*(EXP_WIDTH+1)-1:0] s1_rg_exp_c;\n    generate\n        genvar m;\n        for(m=0;m<N;m++) begin\n            assign s1_rg_exp_c[(m)*(EXP_WIDTH+1) +: (EXP_WIDTH+1)] =\n                $signed(rg_exp_a[(m)*(EXP_WIDTH_I+1) +: (EXP_WIDTH_I+1)]) +\n                $signed(rg_exp_b[(m)*(EXP_WIDTH_I+1) +: (EXP_WIDTH_I+1)]);\n        end\n    endgenerate\n\n    // pairwise maxima (tree level 1): 8 pairs of exponent sums\n    logic signed [(N/2)*(EXP_WIDTH+1)-1:0] s1_exp_pairs;\n    generate\n        genvar h;\n        for(h=0;h<N/2;h++) begin\n            comparator #(.WIDTH(EXP_WIDTH)) u_pair_max (\n                .operand_a(s1_rg_exp_c[(2*h)*(EXP_WIDTH+1) +: (EXP_WIDTH+1)]),\n                .operand_b(s1_rg_exp_c[(2*h+1)*(EXP_WIDTH+1) +: (EXP_WIDTH+1)]),\n                .result_o(s1_exp_pairs[h*(EXP_WIDTH+1) +: (EXP_WIDTH+1)])\n            );\n        end\n    endgenerate\n\n    // balanced popcount of signs (two's complement correction term)\n    logic [15:0] pc1;\n    logic [11:0] pc2;\n    logic [7:0]  pc3;\n    logic [4:0] s1_corr;\n    generate\n        genvar p;\n        for(p=0;p<8;p++) begin\n            assign pc1[2*p +: 2] = {1'b0, s1_signs[2*p]} + {1'b0, s1_signs[2*p+1]};\n        end\n        for(p=0;p<4;p++) begin\n            assign pc2[3*p +: 3] = {1'b0, pc1[4*p +: 2]} + {1'b0, pc1[4*p+2 +: 2]};\n        end\n        for(p=0;p<2;p++) begin\n            assign pc3[4*p +: 4] = {1'b0, pc2[6*p +: 3]} + {1'b0, pc2[6*p+3 +: 3]};\n        end\n    endgenerate\n    assign s1_corr = {1'b0, pc3[3:0]} + {1'b0, pc3[7:4]} + {4'b0, s1_signs[N]};\n\n    // ---- pipe1 ----\n    logic [N:0] pipe1_signs;\n    logic signed [(N/2)*(EXP_WIDTH+1)-1:0] pipe1_exp_pairs;\n    logic signed [EXP_WIDTH_O:0] pipe1_rg_exp_acc;\n    logic [N*(MANT_WIDTH_I+1)-1:0] pipe1_mants_norm_a, pipe1_mants_norm_b;\n    logic [MANT_WIDTH_O:0] pipe1_mant_norm_acc;\n    logic [4:0] pipe1_corr;\n\n    always_ff @(posedge clk) begin\n        pipe1_signs         <= s1_signs;\n        pipe1_exp_pairs     <= s1_exp_pairs;\n        pipe1_rg_exp_acc    <= rg_exp_acc;\n        pipe1_mants_norm_a  <= mants_a;\n        pipe1_mants_norm_b  <= mants_b;\n        pipe1_mant_norm_acc <= mant_norm_acc;\n        pipe1_corr          <= s1_corr;\n    end\n\n    // ============ Stage 2: max-tree tail (9 exps) ============\n    logic signed [(N/2+1)*(EXP_WIDTH+1)-1:0] exp_items2;\n    assign exp_items2[(N/2)*(EXP_WIDTH+1) +: (EXP_WIDTH+1)] = $signed(pipe1_rg_exp_acc);\n    generate\n        genvar t;\n        for(t=0;t<N/2;t++) begin\n            assign exp_items2[t*(EXP_WIDTH+1) +: (EXP_WIDTH+1)] =\n                pipe1_exp_pairs[t*(EXP_WIDTH+1) +: (EXP_WIDTH+1)];\n        end\n    endgenerate\n\n    logic signed [EXP_WIDTH:0] rg_exp_max;\n    comp_tree #(\n        .N(N/2+1),\n        .WIDTH(EXP_WIDTH)\n    ) u_comp_tree (\n        .operands_i(exp_items2),\n        .result_o(rg_exp_max)\n    );\n\n    // ---- pipe2 ----\n    logic [N:0] pipe2_signs;\n    logic signed [(N/2)*(EXP_WIDTH+1)-1:0] pipe2_exp_pairs; // kept for diffs\n    logic signed [EXP_WIDTH:0] pipe2_rg_exp_max;\n    logic [N*(MANT_WIDTH_I+1)-1:0] pipe2_mants_norm_a, pipe2_mants_norm_b;\n    logic [MANT_WIDTH_O:0] pipe2_mant_norm_acc;\n    logic [4:0] pipe2_corr;\n\n    always_ff @(posedge clk) begin\n        pipe2_signs         <= pipe1_signs;\n        pipe2_exp_pairs     <= pipe1_exp_pairs;\n        pipe2_rg_exp_max    <= rg_exp_max;\n        pipe2_mants_norm_a  <= pipe1_mants_norm_a;\n        pipe2_mants_norm_b  <= pipe1_mants_norm_b;\n        pipe2_mant_norm_acc <= pipe1_mant_norm_acc;\n        pipe2_corr          <= pipe1_corr;\n    end\n\n    // ============ Stage 3: mults + diff + align + sign-XOR ============\n    // per-lane exponents recovered from pairwise maxima? NO: the original items\n    // are needed for the diffs. Reconstruct: original per-lane exponents were\n    // replaced by pairwise maxima, which is NOT equivalent for the diffs.\n    // Therefore we must carry the 16 original exponents. Use them here instead:\n    // NOTE: pairwise maxima are only used to shorten the max tree.\n    // We kept the originals implicitly via mants? No -- carry rg_exp_c properly:\n    // (see pipe1_exp_pairs above: only used for max tree)\n\n    // Mantissa products\n    logic [N*MULW-1:0] s3_prod;\n    generate\n        genvar j;\n        for(j=0;j<N;j++) begin\n            assign s3_prod[(j)*MULW +: MULW] =\n                pipe2_mants_norm_a[(j)*(MANT_WIDTH_I+1) +: (MANT_WIDTH_I+1)] *\n                pipe2_mants_norm_b[(j)*(MANT_WIDTH_I+1) +: (MANT_WIDTH_I+1)];\n        end\n    endgenerate\n\n    // Alignment (uses per-lane exponents -- see note above; we need the\n    // originals, which are carried through pipe1/pipe2 as rg_exp_c.)\n    logic [(N+1)*ALIGN_WIDTH-1:0] s3_shifted_xor;\n    generate\n        genvar s;\n        for(s=0;s<N+1;s++) begin : align\n            logic [ALIGN_WIDTH-1:0] product;\n            logic [EXP_WIDTH:0] rg_exp_diff;\n            logic [SHIFT_WIDTH-1:0] shift_amount;\n            logic [ALIGN_WIDTH-1:0] shifted;\n\n            if (s < N) begin\n                assign product = s3_prod[(s)*MULW +: MULW] << (ALIGN_WIDTH-MULW);\n            end\n            else begin\n                assign product = pipe2_mant_norm_acc << (ALIGN_WIDTH-MANT_WIDTH_O-2);\n            end\n\n            assign rg_exp_diff = $unsigned(pipe2_rg_exp_max - rg_exp_items_s[(s)*(EXP_WIDTH+1) +: (EXP_WIDTH+1)]);\n            if(EXP_WIDTH+1>SHIFT_WIDTH) begin\n                assign shift_amount = (|rg_exp_diff[(EXP_WIDTH+1)-1:SHIFT_WIDTH]) ? ALIGN_WIDTH[SHIFT_WIDTH-1:0]\n                                                                                    : rg_exp_diff[SHIFT_WIDTH-1:0];\n            end\n            else begin\n                assign shift_amount = rg_exp_diff[SHIFT_WIDTH-1:0];\n            end\n            assign shifted = product >> shift_amount;\n            assign s3_shifted_xor[(s)*ALIGN_WIDTH +: ALIGN_WIDTH] = shifted ^ {ALIGN_WIDTH{pipe2_signs[s]}};\n        end\n    endgenerate\n\n    // ---- pipe3 ----\n    logic [N:0] pipe3_signs;\n    logic [(N+1)*ALIGN_WIDTH-1:0] pipe3_shifted_xor;\n    logic signed [EXP_WIDTH:0] pipe3_rg_exp_max;\n    logic [4:0] pipe3_corr;\n\n    always_ff @(posedge clk) begin\n        pipe3_signs         <= pipe2_signs;\n        pipe3_shifted_xor   <= s3_shifted_xor;\n        pipe3_rg_exp_max    <= pipe2_rg_exp_max;\n        pipe3_corr          <= pipe2_corr;\n    end\n\n    // ============ Stage 4: sign-extend, CSA tree, 19-bit CPA ============\n    logic [(N+1)*LANE_W-1:0] mantissa_w;\n    generate\n        genvar y;\n        for(y=0;y<N+1;y++) begin\n            assign mantissa_w[(y)*LANE_W +: LANE_W] =\n                { {(LANE_W-ALIGN_WIDTH){pipe3_signs[y]}}, pipe3_shifted_xor[(y)*ALIGN_WIDTH +: ALIGN_WIDTH] };\n        end\n    endgenerate\n\n    logic [LANE_W-1:0] csa_sum, csa_carry;\n    csa_tree #(\n        .N(N+1),\n        .WIDTH_I(LANE_W),\n        .WIDTH_O(LANE_W)\n    ) u_csa_tree (\n        .operands_i(mantissa_w),\n        .sum_o(csa_sum),\n        .carry_o(csa_carry)\n    );\n\n    logic [LANE_W-1:0] sum_result;\n    assign sum_result = csa_sum + csa_carry + {{(LANE_W-5){1'b0}}, pipe3_corr};\n\n    // ---- pipe4 ----\n    logic [LANE_W-1:0] pipe4_sum_result;\n    logic signed [EXP_WIDTH:0] pipe4_rg_exp_max;\n\n    always_ff @(posedge clk) begin\n        pipe4_sum_result <= sum_result;\n        pipe4_rg_exp_max <= pipe3_rg_exp_max;\n    end\n\n    // ============ Stage 5: normalize ============\n    logic final_sign;\n    logic [SUM_WIDTH-1:0] sum_c;\n\n    assign final_sign = pipe4_sum_result[LANE_W-1];\n    assign sum_c = final_sign ? Progress: 820k. Now the critical path is stage 4 (CSA tree + CPA). The 4:2 compressors have a horizontal carry ripple (cin[i+1]=cout[i]) which is costly. Let me make v7 with a pure 3:2-compressor balanced tree and fold `corr` in as an extra CSA operand:


  -> create_file(content="// PDPU dot-16, 5-stage pipelined -- v7.\n// v6 + stage-4 rework:\n//   * balanced 3:2-compressor tree (no horizontal carry ripple, unlike the\n//     4:2 compressors) over 18 operands (17 lanes + popcount correction)\n//   * final 2-input 19-bit CPA only\n//\n// Stages (latency 5):\n//   S1: decode 33 posits, exp adds, pairwise max (8 comps), popcount -> pipe1\n//   S2: max-tree over 9 exps, 9-bit signs                        -> pipe2\n//   S3: diff subtract+clip, align shifts, sign-XOR, 4x4 mults    -> pipe3\n//   S4: sign-extend, 3:2 compressor tree, 19-bit CPA             -> pipe4\n//   S5: abs, LZC, normalize, sticky/round, exp adjust            -> pipe5\n//   encoder: pipe5 -> result_o (combinational)\n\npackage pdpu_pkg;\n    function automatic int maximum(int a, int b);\n        maximum = (a > b) ? a : b;\n    endfunction\n\n    function automatic integer clog2(input integer n);\n        begin\n            n = n-1;\n            for(clog2=0; n>0; clog2=clog2+1)\n                n = n>>1;\n        end\n    endfunction\nendpackage\n\npackage cf_math_pkg;\n    function automatic integer ceil_div (input longint dividend, input longint divisor);\n        longint remainder;\n        remainder = dividend;\n        for (ceil_div = 0; remainder > 0; ceil_div++) begin\n            remainder = remainder - divisor;\n        end\n    endfunction\n\n    function automatic integer clog(input integer n);\n        begin\n            n = n-1;\n            for(clog=0; n>0; clog=clog+1)\n                n = n>>1;\n        end\n    endfunction\n\n    function automatic integer unsigned idx_width (input integer unsigned num_idx);\n        idx_width = (num_idx > 32'd1) ? $unsigned(clog(num_idx)) : 32'd1;\n    endfunction\nendpackage\n\nmodule fulladder(\n    input logic x,y,z,\n    output logic sum,carry\n);\n    assign sum = (x ^ y ) ^ z;\n    assign carry = ((x ^ y) & z) | (x & y);\nendmodule\n\n// Balanced 3:2-compressor (carry-save) tree. All compressor levels are\n// bit-parallel (no cross-bit carry), followed by a single CPA by the user.\n// `operands_i`: flat vector, N operands of WIDTH_I bits.\n// Outputs satisfy: sum of operands == sum_o + carry_o.\nmodule csa_tree32 #(\n    parameter int unsigned N = 8,\n    parameter int unsigned WIDTH_I = 8\n)(\n    input logic [N*WIDTH_I-1:0] operands_i,\n    output logic [WIDTH_I-1:0] sum_o,\n    output logic [WIDTH_I-1:0] carry_o\n);\n    generate\n        if (N == 1) begin : g_one\n            assign sum_o   = operands_i[0 +: WIDTH_I];\n            assign carry_o = '0;\n        end\n        else if (N == 2) begin : g_two\n            assign sum_o   = operands_i[0 +: WIDTH_I];\n            assign carry_o = operands_i[WIDTH_I +: WIDTH_I];\n        end\n        else if (N == 3) begin : g_three\n            genvar b3;\n            for (b3 = 0; b3 < WIDTH_I; b3++) begin : g_fa\n                fulladder u_fa (\n                    .x(operands_i[0*WIDTH_I + b3]),\n                    .y(operands_i[1*WIDTH_I + b3]),\n                    .z(operands_i[2*WIDTH_I + b3]),\n                    .sum(sum_o[b3]),\n                    .carry(carry_o[b3])\n                );\n            end\n        end\n        else begin : g_rec\n            // compress first three operands, recurse on (N-3)+2 = N-1 operands\n            localparam int unsigned N_R = N - 1;\n            logic [WIDTH_I-1:0] s012, c012;\n            logic [(N_R)*WIDTH_I-1:0] rest;\n\n            genvar b;\n            for (b = 0; b < WIDTH_I; b++) begin : g_fa\n                fulladder u_fa (\n                    .x(operands_i[0*WIDTH_I + b]),\n                    .y(operands_i[1*WIDTH_I + b]),\n                    .z(operands_i[2*WIDTH_I + b]),\n                    .sum(s012[b]),\n                    .carry(c012[b])\n                );\n            end\n\n            assign rest = {operands_i[3*WIDTH_I +: (N-3)*WIDTH_I], s012, c012};\n\n            csa_tree32 #(\n                .N(N_R),\n                .WIDTH_I(WIDTH_I)\n            ) u_rec (\n                .operands_i(rest),\n                .sum_o(sum_o),\n                .carry_o(carry_o)\n            );\n        end\n    endgenerate\nendmodule\n\nmodule comparator #(\n    parameter int unsigned WIDTH = 8\n)(\n    input logic signed [WIDTH:0] operand_a,\n    input logic signed [WIDTH:0] operand_b,\n    output logic signed [WIDTH:0] result_o\n);\n    logic [1:0] sign_ab;\n    logic [WIDTH-1:0] data_a;\n    logic [WIDTH-1:0] data_b;\n\n    assign sign_ab = {operand_a[WIDTH],operand_b[WIDTH]};\n    assign data_a = operand_a[WIDTH-1:0];\n    assign data_b = operand_b[WIDTH-1:0];\n\n    always_comb begin\n        case(sign_ab)\n            2'b10: result_o = operand_b;\n            2'b01: result_o = operand_a;\n            default: result_o = (data_a>data_b) ? operand_a : operand_b;\n        endcase\n    end\nendmodule\n\nmodule comp_tree #(\n    parameter N = 4,\n    parameter WIDTH = 8\n)(\n    input logic signed [N*(WIDTH+1)-1:0] operands_i,\n    output logic signed [WIDTH:0] result_o\n);\n    localparam int unsigned W   = WIDTH + 1;\n    localparam int unsigned N_A = N/2;\n    localparam int unsigned N_B = N - N_A;\n\n    generate\n        if (N==1) begin : g_leaf\n            assign result_o = operands_i[0 +: W];\n        end\n        else if (N==2) begin : g_pair\n            comparator #(\n                .WIDTH(WIDTH)\n            ) u_comparator (\n                .operand_a(operands_i[0 +: W]),\n                .operand_b(operands_i[W +: W]),\n                .result_o(result_o)\n            );\n        end\n        else begin : g_split\n            logic signed [N_A*W-1:0] operands_i_A;\n            logic signed [N_B*W-1:0] operands_i_B;\n            logic signed [WIDTH:0]   result_o_A;\n            logic signed [WIDTH:0]   result_o_B;\n\n            assign operands_i_A = operands_i[0       +: N_A*W];\n            assign operands_i_B = operands_i[N_A*W   +: N_B*W];\n\n            comp_tree #(\n                .N(N_A),\n                .WIDTH(WIDTH)\n            ) ua_comp_tree (\n                .operands_i(operands_i_A),\n                .result_o(result_o_A)\n            );\n\n            comp_tree #(\n                .N(N_B),\n                .WIDTH(WIDTH)\n            ) ub_comp_tree (\n                .operands_i(operands_i_B),\n                .result_o(result_o_B)\n            );\n\n            comparator #(\n                .WIDTH(WIDTH)\n            ) uc_comparator (\n                .operand_a(result_o_A),\n                .operand_b(result_o_B),\n                .result_o(result_o)\n            );\n        end\n    endgenerate\nendmodule\n\nmodule barrel_shifter #(\n    parameter int unsigned WIDTH = 8,\n    parameter int unsigned SHIFT_WIDTH = 3,\n    parameter bit MODE = 1'b0\n)(\n    input logic [WIDTH-1:0] operand_i,\n    input logic [SHIFT_WIDTH-1:0] shift_amount,\n    output logic [WIDTH-1:0] result_o\n);\n    logic [SHIFT_WIDTH-1:0][WIDTH-1:0] temp_results;\n\n    assign temp_results[SHIFT_WIDTH-1] = operand_i;\n\n    generate\n        genvar i;\n        if(MODE==1'b0) begin\n            for(i=SHIFT_WIDTH-1;i>0;i--) begin\n                assign temp_results[i-1] = shift_amount[i] ? temp_results[i]<<(2**i) : temp_results[i];\n            end\n            assign result_o = shift_amount[0] ? temp_results[0]<<1 : temp_results;\n        end\n        else begin\n            for(i=SHIFT_WIDTH-1;i>0;i--) begin\n                assign temp_results[i-1] = shift_amount[i] ? temp_results[i]>>(2**i) : temp_results[i];\n            end\n            assign result_o = shift_amount[0] ? temp_results[0]>>1 : temp_results;\n        end\n    endgenerate\nendmodule\n\nmodule lzc #(\n  parameter int unsigned WIDTH = 2,\n  parameter bit          MODE  = 1'b0,\n  parameter int unsigned CNT_WIDTH = cf_math_pkg::idx_width(WIDTH)\n) (\n  input  logic [WIDTH-1:0]     in_i,\n  output logic [CNT_WIDTH-1:0] cnt_o,\n  output logic                 empty_o\n);\n\n  if (WIDTH == 1) begin : gen_degenerate_lzc\n\n    assign cnt_o[0] = !in_i[0];\n    assign empty_o = !in_i[0];\n\n  end else begin : gen_lzc\n\n    localparam int unsigned NumLevels = cf_math_pkg::clog(WIDTH);\n\n    logic [WIDTH*NumLevels-1:0] index_lut;\n    logic [2**NumLevels-1:0] sel_nodes;\n    logic [(2**NumLevels)*NumLevels-1:0] index_nodes;\n\n    logic [WIDTH-1:0] in_tmp;\n\n    always_comb begin : flip_vector\n      for (int unsigned i = 0; i < WIDTH; i++) begin\n        in_tmp[i] = (MODE) ? in_i[WIDTH-1-i] : in_i[i];\n      end\n    end\n\n    for (genvar j = 0; $unsigned(j) < WIDTH; j++) begin : g_index_lut\n      assign index_lut[(j)*NumLevels +: NumLevels] = ($unsigned(j));\n    end\n\n    for (genvar level = 0; $unsigned(level) < NumLevels; level++) begin : g_levels\n      if ($unsigned(level) == NumLevels - 1) begin : g_last_level\n        for (genvar k = 0; k < 2 ** level; k++) begin : g_level\n          if ($unsigned(k) * 2 < WIDTH - 1) begin : g_reduce\n            assign sel_nodes[2 ** level - 1 + k] = in_tmp[k * 2] | in_tmp[k * 2 + 1];\n            assign index_nodes[(2 ** level - 1 + k)*NumLevels +: NumLevels] = (in_tmp[k * 2] == 1'b1)\n              ? index_lut[(k * 2)*NumLevels +: NumLevels] :\n                index_lut[(k * 2 + 1)*NumLevels +: NumLevels];\n          end\n          if ($unsigned(k) * 2 == WIDTH - 1) begin : g_base\n            assign sel_nodes[2 ** level - 1 + k] = in_tmp[k * 2];\n            assign index_nodes[(2 ** level - 1 + k)*NumLevels +: NumLevels] = index_lut[(k * 2)*NumLevels +: NumLevels];\n          end\n          if ($unsigned(k) * 2 > WIDTH - 1) begin : g_out_of_range\n            assign sel_nodes[2 ** level - 1 + k] = 1'b0;\n            assign index_nodes[(2 ** level - 1 + k)*NumLevels +: NumLevels] = '0;\n          end\n        end\n      end else begin : g_not_last_level\n        for (genvar l = 0; l < 2 ** level; l++) begin : g_level\n          assign sel_nodes[2 ** level - 1 + l] =\n              sel_nodes[2 ** (level + 1) - 1 + l * 2] | sel_nodes[2 ** (level + 1) - 1 + l * 2 + 1];\n          assign index_nodes[(2 ** level - 1 + l)*NumLevels +: NumLevels] = (sel_nodes[2 ** (level + 1) - 1 + l * 2] == 1'b1)\n            ? index_nodes[(2 ** (level + 1) - 1 + l * 2)*NumLevels +: NumLevels] :\n              index_nodes[(2 ** (level + 1) - 1 + l * 2 + 1)*NumLevels +: NumLevels];\n        end\n      end\n    end\n\n    assign cnt_o = NumLevels > $unsigned(0) ? index_nodes[(0)*NumLevels +: NumLevels] : {(cf_math_pkg::clog(WIDTH)) {1'b0}};\n    assign empty_o = NumLevels > $unsigned(0) ? ~sel_nodes[0] : ~(|in_i);\n\n  end : gen_lzc\n\nendmodule : lzc\n\nmodule mantissa_norm #(\n    parameter int unsigned WIDTH = 8,\n    parameter int unsigned EXP_WIDTH = 3,\n    parameter int unsigned DECIMAL_POINT = 3\n)(\n    input logic [WIDTH-1:0] operand_i,\n    output logic signed [EXP_WIDTH:0] exp_adjust,\n    output logic [WIDTH-1:0] result_o\n);\n    localparam int unsigned LZC_WIDTH = pdpu_pkg::clog2(WIDTH);\n    logic [LZC_WIDTH-1:0] leading_zero_count;\n    logic lzc_zeroes;\n\n    lzc #(\n        .WIDTH(WIDTH),\n        .MODE(1'b1)\n    ) u_lzc (\n        .in_i(operand_i),\n        .cnt_o(leading_zero_count),\n        .empty_o(lzc_zeroes)\n    );\n\n    always_comb begin\n        if(lzc_zeroes) begin\n            exp_adjust = '0;\n        end\n        else if(leading_zero_count <= DECIMAL_POINT - 1) begin\n            exp_adjust = DECIMAL_POINT - leading_zero_count - 1;\n        end\n        else begin\n            exp_adjust = -$signed(leading_zero_count - DECIMAL_POINT + 1);\n        end\n    end\n\n    barrel_shifter #(\n        .WIDTH(WIDTH),\n        .SHIFT_WIDTH(LZC_WIDTH),\n        .MODE(1'b0)\n    ) u_barrel_shifter (\n        .operand_i(operand_i),\n        .shift_amount(leading_zero_count),\n        .result_o(result_o)\n    );\nendmodule\n\nmodule posit_decoder #(\n    parameter int unsigned n = 16,\n    parameter int unsigned es = 1,\n    parameter int unsigned nd = pdpu_pkg::clog2(n-1),\n    parameter int unsigned EXP_WIDTH = nd+es,\n    parameter int unsigned MANT_WIDTH = n-es-3\n)(\n    input logic [n-1:0] operand_i,\n    output logic sign_o,\n    output logic signed [EXP_WIDTH:0] rg_exp_o,\n    output logic [MANT_WIDTH:0] mant_norm_o\n);\n\n    logic sign;\n    logic [n-2:0] operand_value;\n\n    assign sign = operand_i[n-1];\n    assign operand_value = sign ? (~operand_i[n-2:0]+1) : operand_i[n-2:0];\n\n    logic regS;\n    logic [n-2:0] lzc_operand;\n    logic [nd-1:0] leading_zero_count;\n    logic lzc_zeroes;\n\n    assign regS = operand_value[n-2];\n    assign lzc_operand = regS ? (~operand_value) : operand_value;\n\n    lzc #(\n        .WIDTH(n-1),\n        .MODE(1'b1)\n    ) u_lzc (\n        .in_i(lzc_operand),\n        .cnt_o(leading_zero_count),\n        .empty_o(lzc_zeroes)\n    );\n\n    logic [nd-1:0] runlength;\n    logic [nd-1:0] regime_bits;\n    logic signed [nd:0] regime_k;\n\n    assign runlength = lzc_zeroes ? (n-1) : leading_zero_count;\n    assign regime_bits = lzc_zeroes ? (n-1) : (leading_zero_count+1);\n    assign regime_k = regS ? ({1'b0,runlength-1}) : ({1'b1,~runlength+1});\n\n    logic [n-2:0] op_no_rg;\n\n    barrel_shifter #(\n        .WIDTH(n-1),\n        .SHIFT_WIDTH(nd),\n        .MODE(1'b0)\n    ) u_barrel_shifter (\n        .operand_i(operand_value),\n        .shift_amount(regime_bits),\n        .result_o(op_no_rg)\n    );\n\n    assign sign_o = sign;\n\n    logic [es:0] exp;\n    if(es==0) begin\n        assign exp = 0;\n    end\n    else begin\n        assign exp = op_no_rg[n-2:n-2-es+1];\n    end\n\n    assign rg_exp_o = regime_k << es | exp;\n\n    logic implicit_bit;\n\n    assign implicit_bit = |operand_i[n-2:0];\n    assign mant_norm_o = {implicit_bit, op_no_rg[n-2-es:2]};\nendmodule\n\nmodule posit_encoder #(\n    parameter int unsigned n = 16,\n    parameter int unsigned es = 1,\n    parameter int unsigned nd = pdpu_pkg::clog2(n-1),\n    parameter int unsigned EXP_WIDTH = nd+es,\n    parameter int unsigned MANT_WIDTH = n-es-3\n) (\n    input logic sign_i,\n    input logic signed [EXP_WIDTH:0] rg_exp_i,\n    input logic [MANT_WIDTH:0] mant_norm_i,\n\n    output logic [n-1:0] result_o\n);\n    logic input_not_zero;\n    assign input_not_zero = mant_norm_i[MANT_WIDTH];\n\n    logic signed [EXP_WIDTH-es:0] regime_k;\n    logic signed [es:0] exp;\n\n    assign regime_k = rg_exp_i[EXP_WIDTH:es];\n    if(es==0) begin\n        assign exp = 0;\n    end\n    else begin\n        assign exp = rg_exp_i[es-1:0];\n    end\n\n    logic sign_k;\n    logic [n-2:0] rg_const;\n    logic [n-2:0] regime;\n\n    assign sign_k = rg_exp_i[EXP_WIDTH];\n    assign rg_const = 1;\n    assign regime = sign_k ? rg_const : ~rg_const;\n\n    logic [EXP_WIDTH-es:0] regime_bits;\n\n    assign regime_bits = sign_k ? (~regime_k+2) : (regime_k+2);\n\n    localparam int unsigned TEMP_WIDTH = (n-1)+es+MANT_WIDTH;\n    logic [TEMP_WIDTH-1:0] rg_exp_mant;\n\n    if(es==0) begin\n        assign rg_exp_mant = {regime, mant_norm_i[MANT_WIDTH-1:0]};\n    end\n    else begin\n        assign rg_exp_mant = {regime, exp[es-1:0], mant_norm_i[MANT_WIDTH-1:0]};\n    end\n\n    localparam int unsigned MAX_SHIFT_AMOUNT = MANT_WIDTH + es + 1;\n    localparam int unsigned SHIFT_WIDTH = pdpu_pkg::clog2(MAX_SHIFT_AMOUNT+1);\n    logic [SHIFT_WIDTH-1:0] shift_amount;\n\n    assign shift_amount = (regime_bits>=n) ? MAX_SHIFT_AMOUNT : (regime_bits+(MANT_WIDTH+es-n+1));\n\n    logic [TEMP_WIDTH+MAX_SHIFT_AMOUNT-1:0] value_before_shift, value_after_shift;\n\n    assign value_before_shift = rg_exp_mant << MAX_SHIFT_AMOUNT;\n    barrel_shifter #(\n        .WIDTH(TEMP_WIDTH+MAX_SHIFT_AMOUNT),\n        .SHIFT_WIDTH(SHIFT_WIDTH),\n        .MODE(1'b1)\n    ) u_barrel_shifter (\n        .operand_i(value_before_shift),\n        .shift_amount(shift_amount),\n        .result_o(value_after_shift)\n    );\n\n    logic [n-2:0] value_before_round;\n    logic [MAX_SHIFT_AMOUNT-1:0] rounding_bits;\n\n    assign {value_before_round,rounding_bits} = value_after_shift[MAX_SHIFT_AMOUNT+n-2:0];\n\n    logic round_bit;\n    logic sticky_bit;\n    logic round_value;\n    logic [n-2:0] value_after_round;\n\n    assign round_bit = rounding_bits[MAX_SHIFT_AMOUNT-1];\n    assign sticky_bit = |rounding_bits[MAX_SHIFT_AMOUNT-2:0];\n    assign round_value = round_bit & (sticky_bit | value_before_round[0]);\n    assign value_after_round = value_before_round + round_value;\n\n    logic [n-1:0] normal_result;\n    assign normal_result = sign_i ? {1'b1,~value_after_round+1} : {1'b0,value_after_round};\n    assign result_o = input_not_zero ? normal_result : '0;\n\nendmodule\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Pipelined top\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\nmodule pdpu_top_pipelined #(\n    parameter int unsigned N = 16,\n    parameter int unsigned n_i = 8,\n    parameter int unsigned es_i = 2,\n    parameter int unsigned n_o = 16,\n    parameter int unsigned es_o = 2,\n    parameter int unsigned ALIGN_WIDTH = 14,\n    parameter int unsigned CARRY_W = 5,\n    parameter int unsigned SUM_W = 19\n)(\n    input logic clk,\n    input logic [N*n_i-1:0] operands_a,\n    input logic [N*n_i-1:0] operands_b,\n    input logic [n_o-1:0] acc,\n    output logic [n_o-1:0] result_o\n);\n    localparam int unsigned EXP_WIDTH_I = pdpu_pkg::clog2(n_i-1) + es_i;   // 5\n    localparam int unsigned MANT_WIDTH_I = n_i-es_i-3;                     // 3\n    localparam int unsigned EXP_WIDTH_O = pdpu_pkg::clog2(n_o-1) + es_o;   // 6\n    localparam int unsigned MANT_WIDTH_O = n_o-es_o-3;                     // 11\n    localparam int unsigned EXP_WIDTH = pdpu_pkg::maximum(EXP_WIDTH_I+1,EXP_WIDTH_O); // 6\n    localparam int unsigned MULW = 2*(MANT_WIDTH_I+1);                     // 8\n    localparam int unsigned SUM_WIDTH = ALIGN_WIDTH + CARRY_W;             // 19\n    localparam int unsigned SHIFT_WIDTH = pdpu_pkg::clog2(ALIGN_WIDTH+1);  // 4\n    localparam int unsigned LANE_W = SUM_WIDTH;                            // 19\n\n    // ================= Stage 1: decode, exp adds, pairwise max, popcount =================\n    logic [N-1:0] signs_a, signs_b;\n    logic signed [N*(EXP_WIDTH_I+1)-1:0] rg_exp_a, rg_exp_b;\n    logic [N*(MANT_WIDTH_I+1)-1:0] mants_a, mants_b;\n\n    generate\n        genvar i;\n        for(i=0;i<N;i++) begin: posit_decoding\n            posit_decoder #(.n(n_i),.es(es_i)) ua_posit_decoder (\n                .operand_i(operands_a[(i)*n_i +: n_i]),\n                .sign_o(signs_a[i]),\n                .rg_exp_o(rg_exp_a[(i)*(EXP_WIDTH_I+1) +: (EXP_WIDTH_I+1)]),\n                .mant_norm_o(mants_a[(i)*(MANT_WIDTH_I+1) +: (MANT_WIDTH_I+1)])\n            );\n            posit_decoder #(.n(n_i),.es(es_i)) ub_posit_decoder (\n                .operand_i(operands_b[(i)*n_i +: n_i]),\n                .sign_o(signs_b[i]),\n                .rg_exp_o(rg_exp_b[(i)*(EXP_WIDTH_I+1) +: (EXP_WIDTH_I+1)]),\n                .mant_norm_o(mants_b[(i)*(MANT_WIDTH_I+1) +: (MANT_WIDTH_I+1)])\n            );\n        end\n    endgenerate\n\n    logic sign_acc;\n    logic signed [EXP_WIDTH_O:0] rg_exp_acc;\n    logic [MANT_WIDTH_O:0] mant_norm_acc;\n\n    posit_decoder #(.n(n_o),.es(es_o)) uc_posit_decoder (\n        .operand_i(acc),\n        .sign_o(sign_acc),\n        .rg_exp_o(rg_exp_acc),\n        .mant_norm_o(mant_norm_acc)\n    );\n\n    logic [N-1:0] signs_ab;\n    logic [N:0] s1_signs;\n    assign signs_ab = signs_a ^ signs_b;\n    assign s1_signs = {sign_acc, signs_ab};\n\n    // exponent sums\n    logic signed [N*(EXP_WIDTH+1)-1:0] s1_rg_exp_c;\n    generate\n        genvar m;\n        for(m=0;m<N;m++) begin\n            assign s1_rg_exp_c[(m)*(EXP_WIDTH+1) +: (EXP_WIDTH+1)] =\n                $signed(rg_exp_a[(m)*(EXP_WIDTH_I+1) +: (EXP_WIDTH_I+1)]) +\n                $signed(rg_exp_b[(m)*(EXP_WIDTH_I+1) +: (EXP_WIDTH_I+1)]);\n        end\n    endgenerate\n\n    // pairwise maxima (tree level 1): 8 pairs of exponent sums\n    logic signed [(N/2)*(EXP_WIDTH+1)-1:0] s1_exp_pairs;\n    generate\n        genvar h;\n        for(h=0;h<N/2;h++) begin\n            comparator #(.WIDTH(EXP_WIDTH)) u_pair_max (\n                .operand_a(s1_rg_exp_c[(2*h)*(EXP_WIDTH+1) +: (EXP_WIDTH+1)]),\n                .operand_b(s1_rg_exp_c[(2*h+1)*(EXP_WIDTH+1) +: (EXP_WIDTH+1)]),\n                .result_o(s1_exp_pairs[h*(EXP_WIDTH+1) +: (EXP_WIDTH+1)])\n            );\n        end\n    endgenerate\n\n    // balanced popcount of signs (two's complement correction term)\n    logic [15:0] pc1;\n    logic [11:0] pc2;\n    logic [7:0]  pc3;\n    logic [4:0] s1_corr;\n    generate\n        genvar p;\n        for(p=0;p<8;p++) begin\n            assign pc1[2*p +: 2] = {1'b0, s1_signs[2*p]} + {1'b0, s1_signs[2*p+1]};\n        end\n        for(p=0;p<4;p++) begin\n            assign pc2[3*p +: 3] = {1'b0, pc1[4*p +: 2]} + {1'b0, pc1[4*p+2 +: 2]};\n        end\n        for(p=0;p<2;p++) begin\n            assign pc3[4*p +: 4] = {1'b0, pc2[6*p +: 3]} + {1'b0, pc2[6*p+3 +: 3]};\n        end\n    endgenerate\n    assign s1_corr = {1'b0, pc3[3:0]} + {1'b0, pc3[7:4]} + {4'b0, s1_signs[N]};\n\n    // ---- pipe1 ----\n    logic [N:0] pipe1_signs;\n    logic signed [(N/2)*(EXP_WIDTH+1)-1:0] pipe1_exp_pairs;\n    logic signed [N*(EXP_WIDTH+1)-1:0] pipe1_rg_exp_c;\n    logic signed [EXP_WIDTH_O:0] pipe1_rg_exp_acc;\n    logic [N*(MANT_WIDTH_I+1)-1:0] pipe1_mants_norm_a, pipe1_mants_norm_b;\n    logic [MANT_WIDTH_O:0] pipe1_mant_norm_acc;\n    logic [4:0] pipe1_corr;\n\n    always_ff @(posedge clk) begin\n        pipe1_signs         <= s1_signs;\n        pipe1_exp_pairs     <= s1_exp_pairs;\n        pipe1_rg_exp_c      <= s1_rg_exp_c;\n        pipe1_rg_exp_acc    <= rg_exp_acc;\n        pipe1_mants_norm_a  <= mants_a;\n        pipe1_mants_norm_b  <= mants_b;\n        pipe1_mant_norm_acc <= mant_norm_acc;\n        pipe1_corr          <= s1_corr;\n    end\n\n    // ============ Stage 2: max-tree tail (9 exps) ============\n    logic signed [(N/2+1)*(EXP_WIDTH+1)-1:0] exp_items2;\n    assign exp_items2[(N/2)*(EXP_WIDTH+1) +: (EXP_WIDTH+1)] = $signed(pipe1_rg_exp_acc);\n    generate\n        genvar t;\n        for(t=0;t<N/2;t++) begin\n            assign exp_items2[t*(EXP_WIDTH+1) +: (EXP_WIDTH+1)] =\n                pipe1_exp_pairs[t*(EXP_WIDTH+1) +: (EXP_WIDTH+1)];\n        end\n    endgenerate\n\n    logic signed [EXP_WIDTH:0] rg_exp_max;\n    comp_tree #(\n        .N(N/2+1),\n        .WIDTH(EXP_WIDTH)\n    ) u_comp_tree (\n        .operands_i(exp_items2),\n        .result_o(rg_exp_max)\n    );\n\n    // ---- pipe2 ----\n    logic [N:0] pipe2_signs;\n    logic signed [N*(EXP_WIDTH+1)-1:0] pipe2_rg_exp_c;\n    logic signed [EXP_WIDTH_O:0] pipe2_rg_exp_acc;\n    logic signed [EXP_WIDTH:0] pipe2_rg_exp_max;\n    logic [N*(MANT_WIDTH_I+1)-1:0] pipe2_mants_norm_a, pipe2_mants_norm_b;\n    logic [MANT_WIDTH_O:0] pipe2_mant_norm_acc;\n    logic [4:0] pipe2_corr;\n\n    always_ff @(posedge clk) begin\n        pipe2_signs         <= pipe1_signs;\n        pipe2_rg_exp_c      <= pipe1_rg_exp_c;\n        pipe2_rg_exp_acc    <= pipe1_rg_exp_acc;\n        pipe2_rg_exp_max    <= rg_exp_max;\n        pipe2_mants_norm_a  <= pipe1_mants_norm_a;\n        pipe2_mants_norm_b  <= pipe1_mants_norm_b;\n        pipe2_mant_norm_acc <= pipe1_mant_norm_acc;\n        pipe2_corr          <= pipe1_corr;\n    end\n\n    // ============ Stage 3: mults + diff + align + sign-XOR ============\n    logic [N*MULW-1:0] s3_prod;\n    generate\n        genvar j;\n        for(j=0;j<N;j++) begin\n            assign s3_prod[(j)*MULW +: MULW] =\n                pipe2_mants_norm_a[(j)*(MANT_WIDTH_I+1) +: (MANT_WIDTH_I+1)] *\n                pipe2_mants_norm_b[(j)*(MANT_WIDTH_I+1) +: (MANT_WIDTH_I+1)];\n        end\n    endgenerate\n\n    logic [(N+1)*ALIGN_WIDTH-1:0] s3_shifted_xor;\n    generate\n        genvar s;\n        for(s=0;s<N+1;s++) begin : align\n            logic [ALIGN_WIDTH-1:0] product;\n            logic [EXP_WIDTH:0] rg_exp_diff;\n            logic [SHIFT_WIDTH-1:0] shift_amount;\n            logic [ALIGN_WIDTH-1:0] shifted;\n\n            if (s < N) begin\n                assign product = s3_prod[(s)*MULW +: MULW] << (ALIGN_WIDTH-MULW);\n            end\n            else begin\n                assign product = pipe2_mant_norm_acc << (ALIGN_WIDTH-MANT_WIDTH_O-2);\n            end\n\n            if (s < N) begin\n                assign rg_exp_diff = $unsigned(pipe2_rg_exp_max - pipe2_rg_exp_c[(s)*(EXP_WIDTH+1) +: (EXP_WIDTH+1)]);\n            end\n            else begin\n                assign rg_exp_diff = $unsigned(pipe2_rg_exp_max - $signed(pipe2_rg_exp_acc));\n            end\n            if(EXP_WIDTH+1>SHIFT_WIDTH) begin\n                assign shift_amount = (|rg_exp_diff[(EXP_WIDTH+1)-1:SHIFT_WIDTH]) ? ALIGN_WIDTH[SHIFT_WIDTH-1:0]\n                                                                                    : rg_exp_diff[SHIFT_WIDTH-1:0];\n            end\n            else begin\n                assign shift_amount = rg_exp_diff[SHIFT_WIDTH-1:0];\n            end\n            assign shifted = product >> shift_amount;\n            assign s3_shifted_xor[(s)*ALIGN_WIDTH +: ALIGN_WIDTH] = shifted ^ {ALIGN_WIDTH{pipe2_signs[s]}};\n        end\n    endgenerate\n\n    // ---- pipe3 ----\n    logic [N:0] pipe3_signs;\n    logic [(N+1)*ALIGN_WIDTH-1:0] pipe3_shifted_xor;\n    logic signed [EXP_WIDTH:0] pipe3_rg_exp_max;\n    logic [4:0] pipe3_corr;\n\n    always_ff @(posedge clk) begin\n        pipe3_signs         <= pipe2_signs;\n        pipe3_shifted_xor   <= s3_shifted_xor;\n        pipe3_rg_exp_max    <= pipe2_rg_exp_max;\n        pipe3_corr          <= pipe2_corr;\n    end\n\n    // ============ Stage 4: sign-extend, 3:2 tree, 19-bit CPA ============\n    logic [(N+2)*LANE_W-1:0] mantissa_w;\n    generate\n        genvar y;\n        for(y=0;y<N+1;y++) begin\n            assign mantissa_w[(y)*LANE_W +: LANE_W] =\n                { {(LANE_W-ALIGN_WIDTH){pipe3_signs[y]}}, pipe3_shifted_xor[(y)*ALIGN_WIDTH +: ALIGN_WIDTH] };\n        end\n    endgenerate\n    // correction lane (zero-extended popcount of signs)\n    assign mantissa_w[(N+1)*LANE_W +: LANE_W] = {{(LANE_W-5){1'b0}}, pipe3_corr};\n\n    logic [LANE_W-1:0] csa_sum, csa_carry;\n    csa_tree32 #(\n        .N(N+2),\n        .WIDTH_I(LANE_W)\n    ) u_csa_tree (\n        .operands_i(mantissa_w),\n        .sum_o(csa_sum),\n        .carry_o(csa_carry)\n    );\n\n    logic [LANE_W-1:0] sum_result;\n    assign sum_result = csa_sum + csa_carry;\n\n    // ---- pipe4 ----\n    logic [LANE_W-1:0] pipe4_sum_result;\n    logic signed [EXP_WIDTH:0] pipe4_rg_exp_max;\n\n    always_ff @(posedge clk) begin\n        pipe4_sum_result <= sum_result;\n        pipe4_rg_exp_max <= pipe3_rg_exp_max;\n    end\n\n    // ============ Stage 5: normalize ============\n    logic final_sign;\n    logic [SUM_WIDTH-1:0] sum_c;\n\n    assign final_sign = pipe4_sum_result[LANE_W-1];\n    assign sum_c = final_sign ?   -> edit_file(filename='design_v7.v', new_string="// Balanced 3:2-compressor (carry-save) tree. pipe4 registers both -> stage 5 starts with a plain mux\n//   * final exponent add (rg_exp_max + exp_adjust) moved AFTER pipe5,\n//     right in front of the encoder (off the reg-to-reg critical align shifts, sign-XOR, 4x4 mults       -> pipe3\n//   S4: 3:2 tree (18 lanes), dual CPA (+sum, -sum)         -> pipe4\n//   S5: abs mux, LZC, normalize shift, sticky, exp adjust  -> pipe5\n//   encoder: pipe5 -> result_o (combinational)\n\npackage pdpu_pkg;\n    function automatic int maximum(int a, int b);\n        maximum = (a > b) ? a : b;\n    endfunction\n\n    function automatic integer clog2(input integer n);\n        begin\n            n = n-1;\n            for(clog2=0; n>0; clog2=clog2+1)\n                n = n>>1;\n        end\n    endfunction\nendpackage\n\npackage cf_math_pkg;\n    function automatic integer ceil_div (input longint dividend, input longint divisor);\n        longint remainder;\n        remainder = dividend;\n        for (ceil_div = 0; remainder > 0; ceil_div++) begin\n            remainder = remainder - divisor;\n        end\n    endfunction\n\n    function automatic integer clog(input integer n);\n        begin\n            n = n-1;\n (num_idx > 32'd1) ? $unsigned(clog(num_idx)) : 32'd1;\n    endfunction\nendpackage\n\nmodule fulladder(\n    input logic x,y,z,\n    output logic sum,carry\n);\n    assign sum = (x ^ y ) ^ z;\n    assign carry = ((x ^ y) & z) | (x & y);\nendmodule\n\nmodule comparator #(\n    parameter int unsigned WIDTH = 8\n)(\n    input logic signed [WIDTH:0] operand_a,\n    input logic signed [WIDTH:0] operand_b,\n    output logic signed [WIDTH:0] result_o\n);\n    logic [1:0] sign_ab;\n    logic [WIDTH-1:0] data_a;\n    logic [WIDTH-1:0] data_b;\n\n    assign sign_ab = {operand_a[WIDTH],operand_b[WIDTH]};\n    assign data_a = operand_a[WIDTH-1:0];\n    assign data_b = operand_b[WIDTH-1:0];\n\n    always_comb begin\n        case(sign_ab)\n            2'b10: result_o = operand_b;\n            2'b01: result_o = operand_a;\n            default: result_o = (data_a>data_b) ? operand_a : operand_b;\n        endcase\n    end\nendmodule\n\nmodule comp_tree #(\n    parameter N = 4,\n    parameter WIDTH = 8\n)(\n    input logic signed [N*(WIDTH+1)-1:0] operands_i,\n    output logic signed [WIDTH:0] result_o\n);\n    localparam int unsigned W   = WIDTH + 1;\n    localparam int unsigned N_A = N/2;\n    localparam int unsigned N_B = N - N_A;\n\n    generate\n        if (N==1) begin : g_leaf\n            assign result_o = operands_i[0 +: W];\n        end\n .operand_b(operands_i[W +: W]),\n                .result_o(result_o)\n            );\n        end\n        else begin : g_split\n            logic signed [N_A*W-1:0] operands_i_A;\n            logic (\n                .operands_i(operands_i_B),\n                .result_o(result_o_B)\n            );\n\n            comparator #(\n                .WIDTH(WIDTH)\n            ) uc_comparator (\n                .operand_a(result_o_A),\n                .operand_b(result_o_B),\n                .result_o(result_o)\n            );\n        end\n    endgenerate\nendmodule\n\nmodule barrel_shifter #(\n    parameter int unsigned WIDTH = 8,\n    parameter int unsigned SHIFT_WIDTH = 3,\n    parameter bit MODE = 1'b0\n)(\n    input logic [WIDTH-1:0] operand_i,\n    input logic [SHIFT_WIDTH-1:0] shift_amount,\n    output logic [WIDTH-1:0] result_o\n);\n    logic [SHIFT_WIDTH-1:0][WIDTH-1:0] temp_results;\n\n    assign temp_results[SHIFT_WIDTH-1] = operand_i;\n\n    generate\n        genvar i;\n        if(MODE==1'b0) begin\n            for(i=SHIFT_WIDTH-1;i>0;i--) begin\n                assign temp_results[i-1] = shift_amount[i] ? temp_results[i]<<(2**i) : temp_results[i];\n endgenerate\nendmodule\n\nmodule lzc #(\n  parameter int unsigned WIDTH = 2,\n  parameter bit          MODE  = 1'b0,\n  parameter int unsigned CNT_WIDTH = cf_math_pkg::idx_width(WIDTH)\n) (\n  input + 1];\n            assign index_nodes[(2 ** level - 1 + k)*NumLevels +: NumLevels] = (in_tmp[k * 2] == 1'b1)\n              ? index_lut[(k * 2)*NumLevels +: NumLevels] :\n                index_lut[(k assign sel_nodes[2 ** level - 1 + k] = 1'b0;\n            assign index_nodes[(2 ** level - 1 + k)*NumLevels +: NumLevels] = '0;\n          end\n        end\n      end else begin : g_not_last_level\n l * 2 + 1];\n          assign index_nodes[(2 ** level - 1 + l)*NumLevels +: NumLevels] = (sel_nodes[2 ** (level + 1) - 1 + l * 2] == 1'b1)\n            ? index_nodes[(2 ** (level + 1) - 1 + l * 2)*NumLevels +: NumLevels] :\n              index_nodes[(2 ** (level + 1) - 1 + l * 2 + 1)*NumLevels +: NumLevels];\n        end\n      end\n    end\n\n    assign cnt_o = NumLevels > $unsigned(0) ? index_nodes[(0)*NumLevels +: NumLevels] : {(cf_math_pkg::clog(WIDTH)) {1'b0}};\n    assign empty_o = NumLevels > $unsigned(0) ? ~sel_nodes[0] : ~(|in_i);\n\n  end : gen_lzc\n\nendmodule : lzc\n\nmodule mantissa_norm #(\n    parameter int unsigned WIDTH = 8,\n    parameter int unsigned EXP_WIDTH = 3,\n    parameter int unsigned DECIMAL_POINT = 3\n)(\n    input logic [WIDTH-1:0] operand_i,\n    output logic signed [EXP_WIDTH:0] exp_adjust,\n    output logic [WIDTH-1:0] result_o\n);\n    localparam int unsigned LZC_WIDTH = pdpu_pkg::clog2(WIDTH);\n    logic [LZC_WIDTH-1:0] leading_zero_count;\n    logic lzc_zeroes;\n\n    lzc #(\n        .WIDTH(WIDTH),\n        .MODE(1'b1)\n    ) u_lzc (\n        .in_i(operand_i),\n        .cnt_o(leading_zero_count),\n        .empty_o(lzc_zeroes)\n    );\n\n    always_comb begin\n        if(lzc_zeroes) begin\n            exp_adjust = '0;\n        end\n        else if(leading_zero_count <= DECIMAL_POINT - 1) begin\n            exp_adjust = DECIMAL_POINT - leading_zero_count - 1;\n        end\n        else begin\n            exp_adjust = -$signed(leading_zero_count - DECIMAL_POINT + 1);\n        end\n    end\n\n    barrel_shifter #(\n        .WIDTH(WIDTH),\n        .SHIFT_WIDTH(LZC_WIDTH),\n        .MODE(1'b0)\n    ) u_barrel_shifter (\n        .operand_i(operand_i),\n        .shift_amount(leading_zero_count),\n        .result_o(result_o)\n    );\nendmodule\n\nmodule posit_decoder #(\n    parameter int unsigned n = 16,\n    parameter int unsigned es = 1,\n    parameter int unsigned nd = pdpu_pkg::clog2(n-1),\n    parameter int unsigned EXP_WIDTH = nd+es,\n    parameter int unsigned MANT_WIDTH = n-es-3\n)(\n    input logic [n-1:0] operand_i,\n    output logic sign_o,\n    output logic signed [EXP_WIDTH:0] rg_exp_o,\n    output logic [MANT_WIDTH_O_:0] mant_norm_o\n);\n\n    logic sign;\n    logic [n-2:0] operand_value;\n\n    assign sign = operand_i[n-1];\n    assign operand_value = sign ? (~operand_i[n-2:0]+1) : operand_i[n-2:0];\n\n    logic regS;\n    logic [n-2:0] lzc_operand;\n    logic [nd-1:0] leading_zero_count;\n    logic lzc_zeroes;\n\n    assign regS = operand_value[n-2];\n    assign lzc_operand = regS ? (~operand_value) : operand_value;\n\n    lzc #(\n        .WIDTH(n-1),\n        .MODE(1'b1)\n    ) u_lzc (\n        .in_i(lzc_operand),\n        .cnt_o(leading_zero_count),\n        .empty_o(lzc_zeroes)\n    );\n\n    logic [nd-1:0] runlength;\n    logic [nd-1:0] regime_bits;\n    logic signed [nd:0] regime_k;\n\n    assign runlength = lzc_zeroes ? (n-1) : leading_zero_count;\n    assign regime_bits = lzc_zeroes ? (n-1) : (leading_zero_count+1);\n    assign regime_k = regS ? ({1'b0,runlength-1}) : ({1'b1,~runlength+1});\n\n    logic [n-2:0] op_no_rg;\n\n    barrel_shifter #(\n        .WIDTH(n-1),\n        .SHIFT_WIDTH(nd),\n        .MODE(1'b0)\n    ) u_barrel_shifter (\n        .operand_i(operand_value),\n        .shift_amount(regime_bits),\n        .result_o(op_no_rg)\n    );\n\n    assign sign_o = sign;\n\n    logic [es:0] exp;\n    if(es==0) begin\n        assign exp = 0;\n    end\n    else begin\n        assign exp = op_no_rg[n-2:n-2-es+1];\n    end\n\n    assign rg_exp_o = regime_k << es | exp;\n\n    logic implicit_bit;\n\n    assign implicit_bit = |operand_i[n-2:0];\n    assign mant_norm_o = {implicit_bit, op_no_rg[n-2-es:2]};\nendmodule\n\nmodule posit_encoder #(\n    parameter int unsigned n = 16,\n    parameter int unsigned es = 1,\n    parameter int unsigned nd = pdpu_pkg::clog2(n-1),\n    parameter int unsigned EXP_WIDTH = nd+es,\n    parameter int unsigned MANT_WIDTH = n-es-3\n) (\n    input logic sign_i,\n    input logic signed [EXP_WIDTH:0] rg_exp_i,\n    input logic [MANT_WIDTH:0] mant_norm_i,\n\n    output logic [n-1:0] result_o\n);\n    logic input_not_zero;\n    assign input_not_zero = mant_norm_i[MANT_WIDTH];\n\n    logic signed [EXP_WIDTH-es:0] regime_k;\n    logic signed [es:0] exp;\n\n    assign regime_k = rg_exp_i[EXP_WIDTH:es];\n    if(es==0) begin\n        assign exp = 0;\n    end\n    else begin\n        assign exp = rg_exp_i[es-1:0];\n    end\n\n    logic sign_k;\n    logic [n-2:0] rg_const;\n    logic [n-2:0] regime;\n\n    assign sign_k = rg_exp_i[EXP_WIDTH];\n    assign rg_const = 1;\n    assign regime = sign_k ? rg_const : ~rg_const;\n\n    logic [EXP_WIDTH-es:0] regime_bits;\n\n    assign regime_bits = sign_k ? (~regime_k+2) : (regime_k+2);\n\n    localparam int unsigned TEMP_WIDTH = (n-1)+es+MANT_WIDTH;\n    logic [TEMP_WIDTH-1:0] rg_exp_mant;\n\n    if(es==0) begin\n        assign rg_exp_mant = {regime, mant_norm_i[MANT_WIDTH-1:0]};\n    end\n    else begin\n        assign rg_exp_mant = {regime, exp[es-1:0], mant_norm_i[MANT_WIDTH-1:0]};\n    end\n\n    localparam int unsigned MAX_SHIFT_AMOUNT = MANT_WIDTH + es + 1;\n    localparam int unsigned SHIFT_WIDTH = pdpu_pkg::clog2(MAX_SHIFT_AMOUNT+1);\n    logic [SHIFT_WIDTH-1:0] shift_amount;\n\n    assign shift_amount = (regime_bits>=n) ? MAX_SHIFT_AMOUNT : (regime_bits+(MANT_WIDTH+es-n+1));\n\n    logic [TEMP_WIDTH+MAX_SHIFT_AMOUNT-1:0] value_before_shift, value_after_shift;\n\n    assign value_before_shift = rg_exp_mant << MAX_SHIFT_AMOUNT;\n    barrel_shifter #(\n        .WIDTH(TEMP_WIDTH+MAX_SHIFT_AMOUNT),\n        .SHIFT_WIDTH(SHIFT_WIDTH),\n        .MODE(1'b1)\n    ) u_barrel_shifter (\n        .operand_i(value_before_shift),\n        .shift_amount(shift_amount),\n        .result_o(value_after_shift)\n    );\n\n    logic [n-2:0] value_before_round;\n    logic [MAX_SHIFT_AMOUNT-1:0] rounding_bits;\n\n    assign {value_before_round,rounding_bits} = value_after_shift[MAX_SHIFT_AMOUNT+n-2:0];\n\n    logic round_bit;\n    logic sticky_bit;\n    logic round_value;\n    logic [n-2:0] value_after_round;\n\n    assign round_bit = rounding_bits[MAX_SHIFT_AMOUNT-1];\n    assign sticky_bit = |rounding_bits[MAX_SHIFT_AMOUNT-2:0];\n    assign round_value = round_bit & (sticky_bit | value_before_round[0]);\n    assign value_after_round = value_before_round + round_value;\n\n    logic [n-1:0] normal_result;\n    assign normal_result = sign_i ? {1'b1,~value_after_round+1} : {1'b0,value_after_round};\n    assign result_o = input_not_zero ? normal_result : '0;\n\nendmodule\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Pipelined top\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\nmodule pdpu_top_pipelined #(\n    parameter int unsigned N = 16,\n    parameter int unsigned n_i = 8,\n    parameter int unsigned es_i = 2,\n    parameter int unsigned n_o = 16,\n    parameter int unsigned es_o = 2,\n    parameter int unsigned ALIGN_WIDTH = 14,\n    parameter int unsigned CARRY_W = 5,\n    parameter int unsigned SUM_W = 19\n)(\n    input logic clk,\n    input logic [N*n_i-1:0] operands_a,\n    input logic [N*n_i-1:0] operands_b,\n    input logic [n_o-1:0] acc,\n    output logic [n_o-1:0] result_o\n);\n    localparam int unsigned EXP_WIDTH_I = pdpu_pkg::clog2(n_i-1) + es_i;   // 5\n    localparam int unsigned MANT_WIDTH_I = n_i-es_i-3;                     // 3\n    localparam int unsigned EXP_WIDTH_O = pdpu_pkg::clog2(n_o-1) + es_o;   // 6\n    localparam int unsigned MANT_WIDTH_O = n_o-es_o-3;                     // 11\n    localparam int unsigned EXP_WIDTH = pdpu_pkg::maximum(EXP_WIDTH_I+1,EXP_WIDTH_O); // 6\n    localparam int unsigned MULW = 2*(MANT_WIDTH_I+1);                     // 8\n    localparam int unsigned SUM_WIDTH = ALIGN_WIDTH + CARRY_W;             // 19\n    localparam int unsigned SHIFT_WIDTH = pdpu_pkg::clog2(ALIGN_WIDTH+1);  // 4\n    localparam int unsigned LANE_W = SUM_WIDTH;                            // 19\n\n    // ================= Stage 1: decode, exp adds, pairwise max, popcount =================\n    logic [N-1:0] signs_a, signs_b;\n    logic signed [N*(EXP_WIDTH_I+1)-1:0] rg_exp_a, rg_exp_b;\n    logic [N*(MANT_WIDTH_I+1)-1:0] mants_a, mants_b;\n\n    generate\n        genvar i;\n        for(i=0;i<N;i++) begin: posit_decoding\n            posit_decoder #(.n(n_i),.es(es_i)) ua_posit_decoder (\n                .operand_i(operands_a[(i)*n_i +: n_i]),\n                .sign_o(signs_a[i]),\n                .rg_exp_o(rg_exp_a[(i)*(EXP_WIDTH_I+1) +: (EXP_WIDTH_I+1)]),\n                .mant_norm_o(mants_a[(i)*(MANT_WIDTH_I+1) +: (MANT_WIDTH_I+1)])\n            );\n            posit_decoder #(.n(n_i),.es(es_i)) ub_posit_decoder (\n                .operand_i(operands_b[(i)*n_i +: n_i]),\n                .sign_o(signs_b[i]),\n                .rg_exp_o(rg_exp_b[(i)*(EXP_WIDTH_I+1) +: (EXP_WIDTH_I+1)]),\n                .mant_norm_o(mants_b[(i)*(MANT_WIDTH_I+1) +: (MANT_WIDTH_I+1)])\n            );\n        end\n    endgenerate\n\n    logic sign_acc;\n    logic signed [EXP_WIDTH_O:0] rg_exp_acc;\n    logic [MANT_WIDTH_O:0] mant_norm_acc;\n\n    posit_decoder #(.n(n_o),.es(es_o)) uc_posit_decoder (\n        .operand_i(acc),\n        .sign_o(sign_acc),\n        .rg_exp_o(rg_exp_acc),\n        .mant_norm_o(mant_norm_acc)\n    );\n\n    logic [N-1:0] signs_ab;\n    logic [N:0] s1_signs;\n    assign signs_ab = signs_a ^ signs_b;\n    assign s1_signs = {sign_acc, signs_ab};\n\n    // exponent sums\n    logic signed [N*(EXP_WIDTH+1)-1:0] s1_rg_exp_c;\n    generate\n        genvar m;\n        for(m=0;m<N;m++) begin\n            assign s1_rg_exp_c[(m)*(EXP_WIDTH+1) +: (EXP_WIDTH+1)] =\n                $signed(rg_exp_a[(m)*(EXP_WIDTH_I+1) +: (EXP_WIDTH_I+1)]) +\n                $signed(rg_exp_b[(m)*(EXP_WIDTH_I+1) +: (EXP_WIDTH_I+1)]);\n        end\n    endgenerate\n\n    // pairwise maxima (tree level 1): 8 pairs of exponent sums\n    logic signed [(N/2)*(EXP_WIDTH+1)-1:0] s1_exp_pairs;\n    generate\n        genvar h;\n        for(h=0;h<N/2;h++) begin\n            comparator #(.WIDTH(EXP_WIDTH)) u_pair_max (\n                .operand_a(s1_rg_exp_c[(2*h)*(EXP_WIDTH+1) +: (EXP_WIDTH+1)]),\n                .operand_b(s1_rg_exp_c[(2*h+1)*(EXP_WIDTH+1) +: (EXP_WIDTH+1)]),\n                .result_o(s1_exp_pairs[h*(EXP_WIDTH+1) +: (EXP_WIDTH+1)])\n            );\n        end\n    endgenerate\n\n    // balanced popcount of signs (two's complement correction term)\n    logic [15:0] pc1;\n    logic [11:0] pc2;\n    logic [7:0]  pc3;\n    logic [4:0] s1_corr;\n    generate\n        genvar p;\n        for(p=0;p<8;p++) begin\n            assign pc1[2*p +: 2] = {1'b0, s1_signs[2*p]} + {1'b0, s1_signs[2*p+1]};\n        end\n        for(p=0;p<4;p++) begin\n            assign pc2[3*p +: 3] = {1'b0, pc1[4*p +: 2]} + {1'b0, pc1[4*p+2 +: 2]};\n        end\n        for(p=0;p<2;p++) begin\n            assign pc3[4*p +: 4] = {1'b0, pc2[6*p +: 3]} + {1'b0, pc2[6*p+3 +: 3]};\n        end\n    endgenerate\n    assign s1_corr = {1'b0, pc3[3:0]} + {1'b0, pc3[7:4]} + {4'b0, s1_signs[N]};\n\n    // ---- pipe1 ----\n    logic [N:0] pipe1_signs;\n    logic signed [(N/2)*(EXP_WIDTH+1)-1:0] pipe1_exp_pairs;\n    logic signed [N*(EXP_WIDTH+1)-1:0] pipe1_rg_exp_c;\n    logic signed [EXP_WIDTH_O:0] pipe1_rg_exp_acc;\n    logic [N*(MANT_WIDTH_I+1)-1:0] pipe1_mants_norm_a, pipe1_mants_norm_b;\n    logic [MANT_WIDTH_O:0] pipe1_mant_norm_acc;\n    logic [4:0] pipe1_corr;\n\n    always_ff @(posedge clk) begin\n        pipe1_signs         <= s1_signs;\n        pipe1_exp_pairs     <= s1_exp_pairs;\n        pipe1_rg_exp_c      <= s1_rg_exp_c;\n        pipe1_rg_exp_acc    <= rg_exp_acc;\n        pipe1_mants_norm_a  <= mants_a;\n        pipe1_mants_norm_b  <= mants_b;\n        pipe1_mant_norm_acc <= mant_norm_acc;\n        pipe1_corr          <= s1_corr;\n    end\n\n    // ============ Stage 2: max-tree tail (9 exps) ============\n    logic signed [(N/2+1)*(EXP_WIDTH+1)-1:0] exp_items2;\n    assign exp_items2[(N/2)*(EXP_WIDTH+1) +: (EXP_WIDTH+1)] = $signed(pipe1_rg_exp_acc);\n    generate\n        genvar t;\n        for(t=0;t<N/2;t++) begin\n            assign exp_items2[t*(EXP_WIDTH+1) +: (EXP_WIDTH+1)] =\n                pipe1_exp_pairs[t*(EXP_WIDTH+1) +: (EXP_WIDTH+1)];\n        end\n    endgenerate\n\n    logic signed [EXP_WIDTH:0] rg_exp_max;\n    comp_tree #(\n        .N(N/2+1),\n        .WIDTH(EXP_WIDTH)\n    ) u_comp_tree (\n        .operands_i(exp_items2),\n        .result_o(rg_exp_max)\n    );\n\n    // ---- pipe2 ----\n    logic [N:0] pipe2_signs;\n    logic signed [N*(EXP_WIDTH+1)-1:0] pipe2_rg_exp_c;\n    logic signed [EXP_WIDTH_O:0] pipe2_rg_exp_acc;\n    logic signed [EXP_WIDTH:0] pipe2_rg_exp_max;\n    logic [N*(MANT_WIDTH_I+1)-1:0] pipe2_mants_norm_a, pipe2_mants_norm_b;\n    logic [MANT_WIDTH_O:0] pipe2_mant_norm_acc;\n    logic [4:0] pipe2_corr;\n\n    always_ff @(posedge clk) begin\n        pipe2_signs         <= pipe1_signs;\n        pipe2_rg_exp_c      <= pipe1_rg_exp_c;\n        pipe2_rg_exp_acc    <= pipe1_rg_exp_acc;\n        pipe2_rg_exp_max    <= rg_exp_max;\n        pipe2_mants_norm_a  <= pipe1_mants_norm_a;\n        pipe2_mants_norm_b  <= pipe1_mants_norm_b;\n        pipe2_mant_norm_acc <= pipe1_mant_norm_acc;\n        pipe2_corr          <= pipe1_corr;\n    end\n\n    // ============ Stage 3: mults + diff + align + sign-XOR ============\n    logic [N*MULW-1:0] s3_prod;\n    generate\n        genvar j;\n        for(j=0;j<N;j++) begin\n            assign s3_prod[(j)*MULW +: MULW] =\n                pipe2_mants_norm_a[(j)*(MANT_WIDTH_I+1) +: (MANT_WIDTH_I+1)] *\n                pipe2_mants_norm_b[(j)*(MANT_WIDTH_I+1) +: (MANT_WIDTH_I+1)];\n        end\n    endgenerate\n\n    logic [(N+1)*ALIGN_WIDTH-1:0] s3_shifted_xor;\n    generate\n        genvar s;\n        for(s=0;s<N+1;s++) begin : align\n            logic [ALIGN_WIDTH-1:0] product;\n            logic [EXP_WIDTH:0] rg_exp_diff;\n            logic [SHIFT_WIDTH-1:0] shift_amount;\n            logic [ALIGN_WIDTH-1:0] shifted;\n\n            if (s < N) begin\n                assign product = s3_prod[(s)*MULW +: MULW] << (ALIGN_WIDTH-MULW);\n            end\n            else begin\n                assign product = pipe2_mant_norm_acc << (ALIGN_WIDTH-MANT_WIDTH_O-2);\n            end\n\n            if (s < N) begin\n                assign rg_exp_diff = $unsigned(pipe2_rg_exp_max - pipe2_rg_exp_c[(s)*(EXP_WIDTH+1) +: (EXP_WIDTH+1)]);\n            end\n            else begin\n                assign rg_exp_diff = $unsigned(pipe2_rg_exp_max - $signed(pipe2_rg_exp_acc));\n            end\n            if(EXP_WIDTH+1>SHIFT_WIDTH) begin\n                assign shift_amount = (|rg_exp_diff[(EXP_WIDTH+1)-1:SHIFT_WIDTH]) ? ALIGN_WIDTH[SHIFT_WIDTH-1:0]\n                                                                                    : rg_exp_diff[SHIFT_WIDTH-1:0];\n            end\n            else begin\n                assign shift_amount = rg_exp_diff[SHIFT_WIDTH-1:0];\n            end\n            assign shifted = product >> shift_amount;\n            assign s3_shifted_xor[(s)*ALIGN_WIDTH +: ALIGN_WIDTH] = shifted ^ {ALIGN_WIDTH{pipe2_signs[s]}};\n        end\n    endgenerate\n\n    // ---- pipe3 ----\n    logic [N:0] pipe3_signs;\n    logic [(N+1)*ALIGN_WIDTH-1:0] pipe3_shifted_xor;\n    logic signed [EXP_WIDTH:0] pipe3_rg_exp_max;\n    logic [4:0] pipe3_corr;\n\n    always_ff @(posedge clk) begin\n        pipe3_signs         <= pipe2_signs;\n        pipe3_shifted_xor   <= s3_shifted_xor;\n        pipe3_rg_exp_max    <= pipe2_rg_exp_max;\n        pipe3_corr          <= pipe2_corr;\n    end\n\n    // ============ Stage 4: 3:2 tree + dual CPA (+sum and -sum) ============\n    logic [18*LANE_W-1:0] l1_ops;\n    generate\n        genvar y;\n        for(y=0;y<N+1;y++) begin\n            assign l1_ops[(y)*LANE_W +: LANE_W] =\n                { {(LANE_W-ALIGN_WIDTH){pipe3_signs[y]}}, pipe3_shifted_xor[(y)*ALIGN_WIDTH +: ALIGN_WIDTH] };\n        end\n    endgenerate\n    assign l1_ops[17*LANE_W +: LANE_W] = {{(LANE_W-5){1'b0}}, pipe3_corr};\n\n    logic [12*LANE_W-1:0] l2_ops;\n    logic [8*LANE_W-1:0]  l3_ops;\n    logic [6*LANE_W-1:0]  l4_ops;\n    logic [4*LANE_W-1:0]  l5_ops;\n    logic [3*LANE_W-1:0]  l6_ops;\n\n    generate\n        genvar g1, g2, g3, g4, g5, b1, b2, b3, b4, b5;\n        for (g1 = 0; g1 < 6; g1++) begin : gen_l2\n            logic [LANE_W-1:0] st, ct;\n            for (b1 = 0; b1 < LANE_W; b1++) begin\n                fulladder u_fa (\n                    .x(l1_ops[(3*g1+0)*LANE_W + b1]),\n                    .y(l1_ops[(3*g1+1)*LANE_W + b1]),\n                    .z(l1_ops[(3*g1+2)*LANE_W + b1]),\n                    .sum(st[b1]),\n                    .carry(ct[b1])\n                );\n            end\n            assign l2_ops[(2*g1+0)*LANE_W +: LANE_W] = st;\n            assign l2_ops[(2*g1+1)*LANE_W +: LANE_W] = {ct[LANE_W-2:0], 1'b0};\n        end\n        for (g2 = 0; g2 < 4; g2++) begin : gen_l3\n            logic [LANE_W-1:0] st, ct;\n            for (b2 = 0; b2 < LANE_W; b2++) begin\n                fulladder u_fa (\n                    .x(l2_ops[(3*g2+0)*LANE_W + b2]),\n                    .y(l2_ops[(3*g2+1)*LANE_W + b2]),\n                    .z(l2_ops[(3*g2+2)*LANE_W + b2]),\n                    .sum(st[b2]),\n                    .carry(ct[b2])\n                );\n            end\n            assign l3_ops[(2*g2+0)*LANE_W +: LANE_W] = st;\n            assign l3_ops[(2*g2+1)*LANE_W +: LANE_W] = {ct[LANE_W-2:0], 1'b0};\n        end\n        for (g3 = 0; g3 < 2; g3++) begin : gen_l4\n            logic [LANE_W-1:0] st, ct;\n            for (b3 = 0; b3 < LANE_W; b3++) begin\n                fulladder u_fa (\n                    .x(l3_ops[(3*g3+0)*LANE_W + b3]),\n                    .y(l3_ops[(3*g3+1)*LANE_W + b3]),\n                    .z(l3_ops[(3*g3+2)*LANE_W + b3]),\n                    .sum(st[b3]),\n                    .carry(ct[b3])\n                );\n            end\n            assign l4_ops[(2*g3+0)*LANE_W +: LANE_W] = st;\n            assign l4_ops[(2*g3+1)*LANE_W +: LANE_W] = {ct[LANE_W-2:0], 1'b0};\n        end\n        assign l4_ops[4*LANE_W +: LANE_W] = l3_ops[6*LANE_W +: LANE_W];\n        assign l4_ops[5*LANE_W +: LANE_W] = l3_ops[7*LANE_W +: LANE_W];\n\n        for (g4 = 0; g4 < 2; g4++) begin : gen_l5\n            logic [LANE_W-1:0] st, ct;\n            for (b4 = 0; b4 < LANE_W; b4++) begin\n                fulladder u_fa (\n                    .x(l4_ops[(3*g4+0)*LANE_W + b4]),\n                    .y(l4_ops[(3*g4+1)*LANE_W + b4]),\n                    .z(l4_ops[(3*g4+2)*LANE_W + b4]),\n                    .sum(st[b4]),\n                    .carry(ct[b4])\n                );\n            end\n            assign l5_ops[(2*g4+0)*LANE_W +: LANE_W] = st;\n            assign l5_ops[(2*g4+1)*LANE_W +: LANE_W] = {ct[LANE_W-2:0], 1'b0};\n        end\n        for (g5 = 0; g5 < 1; g5++) begin : gen_l6\n            logic [LANE_W-1:0] st, ct;\n            for (b5 = 0; b5 < LANE_W; b5++) begin\n                fulladder u_fa (\n                    .x(l5_ops[(3*g5+0)*LANE_W + b5]),\n                    .y(l5_ops[(3*g5+1)*LANE_W + b5]),\n                    .z(l5_ops[(3*g5+2)*LANE_W + b5]),\n                    .sum(st[b5]),\n                    .carry(ct[b5])\n                );\n            end\n            assign l6_ops[(2*g5+0)*LANE_W +: LANE_W] = st;\n            assign l6_ops[(2*g5+1)*LANE_W +: LANE_W] = {ct[LANE_W-2:0], 1'b0};\n        end\n        assign l6_ops[2*LANE_W +: LANE_W] = l5_ops[3*LANE_W +: LANE_W];\n    endgenerate\n\n    // final: 3 operands -> 3:2 compress then dual CPA (sum and -sum in parallel)\n    logic [LANE_W-1:0] fs, fc, fc_shift, sum_pos, sum_neg;\n    generate\n        genvar bf;\n        for (bf = 0; bf < LANE_W; bf++) begin : gen_final_fa\n            fulladder u_fa (\n                .x(l6_ops[(0)*LANE_W + bf]),\n                .y(l6_ops[(1)*LANE_W + bf]),\n                .z(l6_ops[(2)*LANE_W + bf]),\n                .sum(fs[bf]),\n                .carry(fc[bf])\n            );\n        end\n    endgenerate\n    assign fc_shift = {fc[LANE_W-2:0], 1'b0};\n    assign sum_pos = fs + fc_shift;             // true sum\n    assign sum_neg = ~fs + ~fc_shift + 1'b1;    // -sum (mod 2^19), in parallel\n\n    // ---- pipe4 ----\n    logic [LANE_W-1:0] pipe4_sum_pos, pipe4_sum_neg;\n    logic signed [EXP_WIDTH:0] pipe4_rg_exp_max;\n\n    always_ff @(posedge clk) begin\n        pipe4_sum_pos    <= sum_pos;\n        pipe4_sum_neg    <= sum_neg;\n        pipe4_rg_exp_max <= pipe3_rg_exp_max;\n    end\n\n    // ============ Stage 5: abs mux, normalize ============\n    logic final_sign;\n    logic [SUM_WIDTH-1:0] sum_c;\n\n    assign final_sign = pipe4_sum_pos[LANE_W-1];\n    assign sum_c = final_sign ? pipe4_sum_neg : pipe4_sum_pos;\n\n    logic signed [EXP_WIDTH:0] rg_exp_adjust;\n    logic [SUM_WIDTH-1:0] sum_norm;\n\n    mantissa_norm #(\n        .WIDTH(SUM_WIDTH),\n        .EXP_WIDTH(EXP_WIDTH),\n        .DECIMAL_POINT(CARRY_W+2)\n    ) u_mantissa_norm (\n        .operand_i(sum_c),\n        .exp_adjust(rg_exp_adjust),\n        .result_o(sum_norm)\n    );\n\n    logic [MANT_WIDTH_O+2:0] final_mant;\n    if(SUM_WIDTH>MANT_WIDTH_O+3) begin\n        logic [SUM_WIDTH-MANT_WIDTH_O-3:0] sticky_bits;\n        logic sticky_bit;\n        assign sticky_bits = sum_norm[SUM_WIDTH-MANT_WIDTH_O-3:0];\n        assign sticky_bit = |sticky_bits;\n        assign final_mant = {sum_norm[SUM_WIDTH-1:SUM_WIDTH-MANT_WIDTH_O-2],sticky_bit};\n    end\n    else begin\n        assign final_mant = sum_norm << (MANT_WIDTH_O+3-SUM_WIDTH);\n    end\n\n    // ---- pipe5 ----\n    logic pipe5_final_sign;\n    logic signed [EXP_WIDTH:0] pipe5_rg_exp_max;\n    logic signed [EXP_WIDTH:0] pipe5_rg_exp_adjust;\n    logic [MANT_WIDTH_O+2:0] pipe5_final_mant;\n\n    always_ff @(posedge clk) begin\n        pipe5_final_sign     <= final_sign;\n        pipe5_rg_exp_max     <= pipe4_rg_exp_max;\n        pipe5_rg_exp_adjust  <= rg_exp_adjust;\n        pipe5_final_mant     <= final_mant;\n    end\n\n    // ============ Encoder (combinational, after pipe5) ============\n    logic signed [EXP_WIDTH:0] final_rg_exp;\n    assign final_rg_exp = pipe5_rg_exp_max + pipe5_rg_exp_adjust;\n\n    posit_encoder #(\n        .n(n_o),\n        .es(es_o),\n        .EXP_WIDTH(EXP_WIDTH),\n        .MANT_WIDTH(MANT_WIDTH_O+2)\n    ) u_posit_encoder (\n        .sign_i(pipe5_final_sign),\n        .rg_exp_i(final_rg_exp),\n        .mant_norm_i(pipe5_final_mant),\n        .result_o(result_o)\n    );\n\nendmodule\n", filename='design_v8.v')

[ASSISTANT]
The champion is v7 (736k) with stage 5 (763ps) as the bottleneck, ending at `pipe5_final_rg_exp` (the LZC→exp-adjust→add chain). Let me build v9 with two targeted fixes:

**(A)** Run LZC in parallel with the negation: `LZC(|x|) = LZC(x XOR signext) - [x is negative power of two]` (a 1-bit correction), shortening the critical exp path.

**(B)** Encoder: replace the 46-bit right barrel shifter with a 31-bit left "window" shifter (mathematically identical window extraction).


  -> create_file(content='// PDPU dot-16, 5-stage pipelined -- v9.\n// v7 + stage-5 critical-path surgery:\n//   * LZC runs on (sum XOR sign-extended sign) -- one XOR layer off the\n//     registers -- in parallel with the magnitude increment. A 1-bit\n//     correction handles the only discrepancy: sums that are negative\n//     powers of two (where the +1 carry reaches the top of ~x).\n//   * encoder: 31-bit left "window" shifter replaces LZC+corr, normalize shift, sticky, exp adjust  -> pipe5\n//   encoder: pipe5 -> result_o (combinational)\n\npackage pdpu_pkg;\n    function automatic int maximum(int a, int b);\n        maximum = (a > b) ? a : b;\n    endfunction\n\n    function automatic integer clog2(input integer n);\n        begin\n            n = n-1;\n            for(clog2=0; n>0; clog2=clog2+1)\n                n = n>>1;\n dividend;\n        for (ceil_div = 0; remainder > 0; ceil_div++) begin\n            remainder = remainder - divisor;\n        end\n    endfunction\n\n    function automatic integer clog(input integer n);\n        begin\n            n = n-1;\n            for(clog=0; n>0; clog=clog+1)\n                n = n>>1;\n        end\n    endfunction\n\n    function automatic integer unsigned idx_width (input integer unsigned num_idx);\n        idx_width = (num_idx > 32\'d1) ? $unsigned(clog(num_idx)) : 32\'d1;\n    endfunction\nendpackage\n\nmodule fulladder(\n    input logic x,y,z,\n    output logic sum,carry\n);\n    assign sum = (x ^ y ) ^ z;\n    assign carry = ((x ^ y) & z) | (x & y);\nendmodule\n\nmodule comparator #(\n    parameter int unsigned WIDTH = 8\n)(\n    input logic signed .operand_a(operands_i[0 +: W]),\n                .operand_b(operands_i[W +: W]),\n                .result_o(result_o)\n            );\n        end\n        else begin : g_split\n            logic +: NumLevels] = \'0;\n          end\n        end\n      end else begin : g_not_last_level\n        for (genvar l = 0; l < 2 ** level; l++) begin : g_level\n          assign sel_nodes[2 ** level - 1 +
